```
I now have a thorough understanding of the codebase. Let me compile my findings.

---

Here is the efficiency review of the Recycle Bin feature diff:

## 1. Redundant `deriveBinIpnsKeypair` calls (repeated HKDF + Ed25519 crypto on every operation)

**File:** `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/services/bin.service.ts`, lines 54, 81, 141

`deriveBinIpnsKeypair` performs HKDF-SHA256 + Ed25519 public key derivation + IPNS name derivation. It is called in three places:

- `loadBinMetadata()` (line 54) -- called by `initializeBin`
- `saveBinMetadata()` (line 81) -- called by every write operation
- `initializeBin()` (line 141) -- calls `deriveBinIpnsKeypair` itself, then immediately calls `loadBinMetadata` which derives it again

So during `initializeBin`, the IPNS keypair is derived **twice** (once at line 141, once inside `loadBinMetadata` at line 54). During `addToBin`, it is derived once in `saveBinMetadata`. The result is deterministic and never changes for a given private key -- it should be derived once and cached (or at least passed through).

## 2. Sequential N+1 pattern in batch delete operations (`emptyBin` and `purgeExpired`)

**File:** `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/services/bin.service.ts`, lines 342-348, 391-397

Both `emptyBin` and `purgeExpired` iterate entries with `for...of` + `await cleanupEntryCids(entry)`, performing sequential IPNS resolution + IPFS fetch + unpin for each entry. For a bin with N file entries, this is at minimum 3N sequential network requests (resolve IPNS, fetch metadata, unpin CID) when these are independent operations that could run concurrently with `Promise.allSettled`.

## 3. Sequential N+1 pattern in batch restore/delete in `useBin` hook

**File:** `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/hooks/useBin.ts`, lines 134-139, 158-163

`restoreMultiple` and `permanentDeleteMultiple` loop over entry IDs with `for...of` + `await`. Each iteration of `restoreFromBin` or `permanentlyDelete` calls `removeEntriesFromBin` which does a full encrypt + IPFS upload + IPNS publish cycle. For N items, this performs N separate IPNS publishes when a single batch operation (remove all, publish once) would suffice.

## 4. Redundant IPNS publish per item in batch addToBin (from batch delete)

**File:** `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/hooks/useFolderMutations.ts`, lines 747-756

When batch-deleting files, each deleted child fires `void addToBin(...)` independently. Each `addToBin` call reads the current store state, appends one entry, encrypts the whole metadata, uploads to IPFS, and publishes an IPNS record. For N items deleted together, this creates N race conditions (all reading the same initial store state) and N sequential IPNS publishes. Ideally there would be a batch `addMultipleToBin` that appends all entries and publishes once.

## 5. Linear scan for folder lookup by IPNS name (`Object.values().find()`)

**File:** `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/services/bin.service.ts`, lines 488, 548

Both `cleanupFolderCids` (line 488) and `resolveTargetFolder` (line 548) do `Object.values(folders).find((f) => f.ipnsName === ...)` which is O(N) over all loaded folders. This is called recursively in `cleanupFolderCids` for every subfolder in a deleted folder tree. If the folder store maintained an IPNS-name index (a `Map<string, string>` from ipnsName to folder ID), these lookups would be O(1).

## 6. Sequential file cleanup inside `cleanupFolderCids` recursive traversal

**File:** `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/services/bin.service.ts`, lines 492-518

For each child file in a folder, the code sequentially resolves the file IPNS record, fetches the metadata from IPFS, parses JSON, unpins the content CID, then unpins the metadata CID. These per-file operations are independent and could run concurrently. For a folder tree with many files, this is extremely slow due to serial network calls.

## 7. `cleanupFolderCids` silently skips unloaded folders (partial cleanup)

**File:** `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/services/bin.service.ts`, lines 487-489

`cleanupFolderCids` looks for the folder in the in-memory store. If the folder is not loaded (which is the common case for deep folder trees that haven't been navigated to), the function only unpins the folder metadata CID (line 522) but skips all descendant files. Those CIDs remain pinned indefinitely, leaking storage quota. This is not strictly an efficiency issue but a correctness/resource-leak issue worth noting.

## 8. Duplicate file cleanup logic (code duplication between file and folder paths)

**File:** `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/services/bin.service.ts`, lines 446-468 vs 496-509

The logic for resolving a file IPNS record, fetching metadata, parsing JSON, unpinning content CID, and updating quota is duplicated between `cleanupEntryCids` (for top-level file entries) and `cleanupFolderCids` (for nested file children). This is a maintenance burden and increases the code surface for bugs. A shared helper like `unpinFileCids(fileMetaIpnsName)` would eliminate the duplication.

## 9. `daysRemaining` called per-row during sort comparisons

**File:** `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/components/file-browser/BinBrowser.tsx`, line 94 (sort comparator) + line 438 (render prop)

When sorting by `daysRemaining`, the `daysRemaining(entry)` function is called O(N log N) times during sort because it is called inside the sort comparator. Then it is called again for each row in the render pass (line 438: `daysLeft={daysRemaining(entry)}`). The function itself does `Date.now()` + arithmetic each time. For large bin lists, pre-computing a `Map<string, number>` of days remaining before sorting would avoid redundant `Date.now()` calls and arithmetic.

## 10. ECIES `wrap_key` in FUSE `rmdir` hot path (Rust)

**File:** `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/fuse/write_ops.rs`, lines 650-656

During `handle_rmdir`, the code calls `crate::crypto::ecies::wrap_key(key, &fs.public_key)` to ECIES-encrypt the IPNS private key for the `FolderEntry` in the bin entry. This is an elliptic curve operation on the FUSE callback thread. Per the CLAUDE.md FUSE architecture section: "ALL FUSE callbacks run on a single thread. Any blocking call stalls the entire filesystem." While ECIES wrapping is fast (sub-millisecond), it is still a crypto operation on the single FUSE thread where the guidance says to avoid non-trivial work. The `spawn_bin_entry_publish` background thread could do this wrapping instead, since it already does ECIES encryption of the bin metadata.

## 11. Redundant `SystemTime::now()` calls in write_ops

**File:** `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/fuse/write_ops.rs`, lines 281-283 (unlink) and 640-643 (rmdir)

In `handle_unlink`, `SystemTime::now()` is called at line 281 (for `now_ms`), then again at line 343 (for `deleted_at`). These could use the same timestamp. Similarly in `handle_rmdir`, `SystemTime::now()` at line 640 and again at line 717. Minor, but produces subtly inconsistent timestamps on the same operation.

## 12. `to_ascii_lowercase()` allocates a new String on every MIME type lookup (Rust)

**File:** `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/crypto/bin.rs`, lines 371-376

`guess_mime_type` calls `.to_ascii_lowercase()` on the extension, which allocates a new `String` heap object for every file delete. Since this runs on the FUSE thread during `handle_unlink`, a stack-based approach (e.g., `eq_ignore_ascii_case` comparisons or a small inline buffer) would avoid the allocation.

## 13. Module-level mutable state for TEE enrollment (`binTeeEnrolled`)

**File:** `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/services/bin.service.ts`, line 38

`let binTeeEnrolled = false` is a module-level mutable boolean. It is reset in `initializeBin` (line 139) and checked/set in `saveBinMetadata` (lines 93-104). If the user opens multiple tabs, each tab has its own module scope so this is safe. However, if `saveBinMetadata` is called concurrently (e.g., rapid batch deletes), multiple concurrent calls could all see `binTeeEnrolled === false` and all attempt TEE enrollment simultaneously, doing redundant ECIES wrap operations.

## 14. `sortIndicator` function re-created every render

**File:** `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/components/file-browser/BinBrowser.tsx`, lines 116-119

`sortIndicator` is defined as a regular function inside the component body (not wrapped in `useCallback` or `useMemo`). It is called 4 times in the JSX for column headers. While this is a minor allocation, it contrasts with the careful `useCallback` wrapping of other handlers in the same component. This is a very minor issue.

## 15. Unbounded bin metadata growth

**File:** `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/packages/crypto/src/bin/types.ts`, line 58 (`entries: BinEntry[]`)

The `RecycleBinMetadata` contains an unbounded `entries` array. Every delete appends to it; entries are only removed by explicit purge/restore/permanent-delete. If a user deletes thousands of files over 30 days without purging, the entire metadata blob (which is encrypted as one ECIES-wrapped JSON) grows without limit. There is no pagination or size cap. Each `addToBin` or `saveBinMetadata` call re-encrypts and re-uploads the entire blob. With large bins, this could become a significant performance bottleneck for every bin mutation.

## 16. `getConfig` re-parses env var on every API call

**File:** `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/api/src/vault/vault.service.ts`, lines 39-47

`getConfig()` calls `configService.get('RECYCLE_BIN_RETENTION_DAYS')`, does `Number()` parsing, and validates on every request. While NestJS `ConfigService` caches env vars, the `Number()` + validation is repeated. This value never changes at runtime, so it could be computed once in the constructor and stored as a property. Very minor since this endpoint is called infrequently.

## Summary of highest-impact findings

The most significant efficiency issues are:

1. **Finding 2 + 3 + 4 (sequential N+1 patterns)**: Batch operations serialize network calls that could be concurrent, and each item in a batch triggers a full encrypt+upload+publish cycle instead of batching.
2. **Finding 15 (unbounded metadata blob)**: Every bin mutation re-encrypts and re-uploads the entire entry list, which grows without bound.
3. **Finding 7 (silent skip of unloaded folders)**: `cleanupFolderCids` silently skips descendant cleanup when folders are not in memory, causing permanent storage leaks.
4. **Finding 1 (redundant HKDF derivation)**: Deterministic crypto derivation is repeated unnecessarily across calls.
5. **Finding 5 (linear folder scan)**: O(N) folder lookup by IPNS name is called recursively during cleanup.
```

```
I now have a comprehensive picture. Here is my full review:

---

## Code Review: Recycle Bin Feature (Phase 17)

### 1. Redundant State

**1a. `useBin` hook duplicates `isLoading` from store AND from local `useState`**
- `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/hooks/useBin.ts`, lines 20-23
- `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/stores/bin.store.ts`, lines 8, 25, 85
- The hook creates its own `useState<{ isLoading: boolean; error: string | null }>` (line 20) while the bin store also has `isLoading` (line 8) and `error` (line 11) state with corresponding `setLoading` and `setError` actions. The service layer (`bin.service.ts` line 135) also calls `store.setLoading(true)` in `initializeBin`. This means there are two independent `isLoading` flags: one in the Zustand store and one in the hook's local React state. They can go out of sync -- for example, `initializeBin` sets the store's `isLoading` to `true` but the hook independently manages its own `isLoading` in `loadBin`. The store's `setLoading` and `setError` are called from the service but never consumed by the hook (which reads its own local state instead).

**1b. Bin store has `setLoaded` action that is never called by anyone**
- `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/stores/bin.store.ts`, line 26 (type), line 87 (implementation)
- The `setLoaded` action is defined but never called externally. `isLoaded` is only ever set to `true` inside `setEntries` (line 59) and reset to `false` in `clearBin` (line 103). This is dead code.

**1c. `addToBin` double-counts the sequence number increment**
- `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/services/bin.service.ts`, lines 202-215
- At line 203, `newSeq = store.sequenceNumber + 1` is computed for the metadata to publish. Then at line 215, `store.addEntry(entry)` is called, which internally increments `sequenceNumber` again (store line 67: `sequenceNumber: state.sequenceNumber + 1`). So after `addToBin`, the local store's `sequenceNumber` is `original + 2` while the published metadata says `original + 1`. On the next operation, the metadata will be built from the store's inflated sequence number, creating a gap.

### 2. Parameter Sprawl

**2a. `userPublicKey` + `userPrivateKey` passed through every public function**
- `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/services/bin.service.ts`, lines 131-132, 176-182, 230-234, 305-309, 332-337, 374-378
- `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/hooks/useBin.ts`, lines 38-41, 69-73, 91-95, 113-116, 134-139, 158-163
- Every single public API function (`initializeBin`, `addToBin`, `restoreFromBin`, `permanentlyDelete`, `emptyBin`, `purgeExpired`) and every hook wrapper takes `{ userPublicKey, userPrivateKey }`. The hook then does `auth.vaultKeypair.publicKey` / `auth.vaultKeypair.privateKey` at every call site identically. The device-registry service (`device-registry.service.ts`) follows the same pattern, so this is consistent with existing code, but it creates 6 identical `useAuthStore.getState()` extractions in the hook and 6 identical `throw new Error('Not authenticated')` guards.

**2b. `spawn_bin_entry_publish` takes 6 separate parameters**
- `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/fuse/mod.rs`, lines 810-816
- The function signature is `(api, rt, entry, user_private_key, user_public_key, coordinator)`. Compare with `spawn_metadata_publish` which takes a similar set. These could share a context struct (e.g., `BinPublishContext`) since the same set of fields (`api`, `rt`, `private_key`, `public_key`, `publish_coordinator`) is extracted from `CipherBoxFS` at both call sites.

### 3. Copy-Paste with Slight Variation

**3a. File CID cleanup logic is duplicated between `cleanupEntryCids` and `cleanupFolderCids`**
- `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/services/bin.service.ts`, lines 443-473 vs. lines 492-518
- Both contain an identical 12-line block: resolve file IPNS name -> fetch metadata -> parse JSON via `new TextDecoder().decode(metaBytes)` -> `JSON.parse` -> check `fileMeta.cid` -> `unpinFromIpfs` -> check `fileMeta.size` -> `removeUsage` -> unpin metadata CID. The only difference is error log message text. This should be extracted into a shared `cleanupFileCid(fileMetaIpnsName)` helper.

**3b. `handle_unlink` and `handle_rmdir` bin-entry creation blocks are near-identical**
- `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/fuse/write_ops.rs`, lines 326-361 vs. lines 699-735
- Both blocks follow the same pattern: (1) extract `parent_ipns_name` from parent inode with the same match on `InodeKind::Root`/`InodeKind::Folder`, (2) call `build_folder_path(fs, parent)`, (3) construct `BinEntry` with almost identical fields, (4) call `spawn_bin_entry_publish` with the same 6 arguments. The parent IPNS name extraction (lines 327-333 and 701-707) is a verbatim copy. A shared helper like `extract_parent_ipns_name(fs, parent_ino) -> String` would eliminate this.

**3c. `RecycleBinMetadata` construction with `version: 'v1'` is repeated 3 times**
- `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/services/bin.service.ts`, lines 205-209, 352-356, 420-424
- Each time a metadata object is constructed, the same `version: 'v1'` literal is hardcoded. The Rust side has `empty_bin_metadata()` as a constructor, but the TypeScript side has no equivalent factory. A helper like `createBinMetadata(entries, seq)` would centralize the version string.

**3d. `useBin` hook callbacks follow an identical try/catch/setState pattern**
- `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/hooks/useBin.ts`, lines 63-80, 85-102, 107-123, 128-147, 152-171
- Five callbacks (`restore`, `permanentDelete`, `emptyAll`, `restoreMultiple`, `permanentDeleteMultiple`) all follow the exact same structure: (1) get auth, (2) guard for `vaultKeypair`, (3) `setState({ isLoading: true, error: null })`, (4) try { await serviceCall(...) } catch { setState error; throw }, (5) `setState({ isLoading: false, error: null })`. This could be a generic `wrapBinOperation(serviceCall)` higher-order function.

### 4. Leaky Abstractions

**4a. `bin.service.ts` directly reads file metadata by parsing raw JSON, bypassing the existing `decryptFileMetadata` crypto module**
- `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/services/bin.service.ts`, lines 450-461 and 499-508
- The cleanup code does `new TextDecoder().decode(metaBytes)` then `JSON.parse(metaJson)` to access `fileMeta.cid` and `fileMeta.size`. But file metadata is encrypted with AES-GCM (using the file key). This raw JSON parse would only work if the metadata blob is unencrypted, which contradicts the encryption architecture. If file metadata IS encrypted, this code silently fails. If it IS somehow plaintext JSON, then using `JSON.parse` bypasses the schema validation that exists in `@cipherbox/crypto`. Either way, the code is either broken or breaks the abstraction boundary of the file metadata module.

**4b. `restoreFromBin` dynamically imports `folder.service` to avoid circular dependency**
- `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/services/bin.service.ts`, line 254
- `const { updateFolderMetadata } = await import('./folder.service');` uses a dynamic import to avoid a circular dependency. This is a symptom of bin.service reaching directly into folder mutation internals rather than going through a shared service boundary or event system.

**4c. `getRootFolder` constructs a `FolderNode` with dummy crypto keys**
- `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/services/bin.service.ts`, lines 596-608
- The fallback path creates a `FolderNode` with `folderKey: new Uint8Array(32)` and `ipnsPrivateKey: new Uint8Array(64)` (same pattern at line 285-286 for restored folder placeholders). These are zeroed-out placeholder keys that would produce corrupted encryption if accidentally used.

**4d. `cleanupFolderCids` can only clean folders that are loaded in the store**
- `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/services/bin.service.ts`, lines 487-489
- `const folderNode = Object.values(folders).find((f) => f.ipnsName === folderIpnsName);` only finds folders already loaded in the Zustand store. If the folder was deleted but not previously navigated to, the cleanup silently does nothing. The function does not attempt to decrypt the folder metadata using the folder key (which IS available in the bin entry's `folderEntry.folderKeyEncrypted`). This creates a leak where CIDs for unvisited deleted folders are never unpinned.

### 5. Stringly-Typed Code

**5a. `itemType: 'file' | 'folder'` string literal in BinEntry used alongside `item.type === 'file'` checks with no shared constant**
- `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/services/bin.service.ts`, line 189
- `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/packages/crypto/src/bin/types.ts`, line 23
- `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/packages/crypto/src/bin/schema.ts`, line 12
- The `BinEntry.itemType` uses raw `'file' | 'folder'` strings. The schema validator at line 12 defines `const VALID_ITEM_TYPES = ['file', 'folder']` as a runtime array. The existing codebase elsewhere uses the same raw string pattern (e.g., `FolderChild` has `type: 'file' | 'folder'`), so this is consistent with the codebase, but the Rust side defines `BinItemType` as a proper enum (`BinItemType::File | BinItemType::Folder`) while the TypeScript side uses raw strings. A shared `BinItemType` union type exported from `types.ts` would be more self-documenting.

**5b. `version: 'v1'` hardcoded as a string literal in 3 places in TypeScript and 2 in Rust**
- `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/services/bin.service.ts`, lines 206, 353, 421
- `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/crypto/bin.rs`, lines 337, 349
- `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/packages/crypto/src/bin/types.ts`, line 54 (type literal `'v1'`)
- The version is `'v1'` in the TypeScript type definition (which is good -- it's a literal type), but the service constructs metadata objects with raw `'v1'` strings rather than a constant. The Rust side checks `metadata.version != "v1"` as a raw string. A `BIN_METADATA_VERSION` constant would be more maintainable.

**5c. `BinBrowser.tsx` destructures `isLoading` twice with alias, creating confusing naming**
- `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/components/file-browser/BinBrowser.tsx`, lines 23 and 34
- The hook returns `isLoading` which is destructured at line 23, but then destructured again at line 34 as `isLoading: isOperating`. Since the `useBin` hook spreads `...state` which includes `isLoading`, the second destructure shadows the first. The resulting code uses `isOperating` for button disabled states but `isLoading` for the loading spinner. Both refer to the same hook-level `isLoading` boolean, making the aliasing misleading (it implies they track different things).

### Additional Notes

**Sequencing issue in `addToBin`** (related to finding 1c): The function at `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/services/bin.service.ts` line 201-215 builds the full metadata with `currentEntries = [...store.entries, entry]` and `newSeq = store.sequenceNumber + 1`, publishes it, then calls `store.addEntry(entry)`. But `addEntry` (bin.store.ts line 64-68) does `entries: [...state.entries, entry], sequenceNumber: state.sequenceNumber + 1`. So after execution: published metadata has `seq = N+1` with the new entry, but the store has `seq = N+2` with the new entry. The next `removeEntriesFromBin` call (line 418) reads `binStore.sequenceNumber` which is now `N+2` and increments to `N+3`, creating a permanent gap.

**Rust `generate_uuid_v4` is duplicated from `registry/mod.rs`**

- `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/registry/mod.rs`, lines 152-165
```

```
### Items that intentionally follow established patterns (NOT flagged as issues)

The following items look structurally similar to existing code but are domain-specific implementations that correctly follow the established patterns in the codebase. They are not duplications worth refactoring:

- **`packages/crypto/src/bin/derive-ipns.ts`** -- follows the exact same pattern as `registry/derive-ipns.ts` and `vault/derive-ipns.ts` but with a different HKDF info string. This is the intended architecture: each IPNS domain gets its own `derive-ipns.ts` with its own HKDF info for domain separation.

- **`packages/crypto/src/bin/encrypt.ts`** -- mirrors `registry/encrypt.ts` exactly. Both are thin wrappers around `wrapKey`/`unwrapKey` with JSON serialization and schema validation. Abstracting these into a generic "ECIES-encrypt-any-metadata" helper would reduce lines but hurt readability since each module has its own types and validation.

- **`packages/crypto/src/bin/schema.ts`** -- follows the same manual validation pattern as `registry/schema.ts`. Domain-specific validation logic that must differ per metadata type.

- **`apps/web/src/stores/bin.store.ts`** -- follows the same Zustand store pattern as `device-registry.store.ts`. Each store manages different state shapes.

- **`apps/web/src/services/bin.service.ts`** -- follows the same IPFS/IPNS lifecycle pattern as `device-registry.service.ts` (`derive IPNS -> resolve -> fetch -> decrypt -> modify -> encrypt -> pin -> publish`). The steps are inherently the same because they use the same infrastructure, but the business logic differs.

- **`apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/crypto/bin.rs` encrypt/decrypt** -- follows the same ECIES pattern as the registry module in Rust. The HKDF derivation in `hkdf.rs` correctly reuses the existing `derive_ipns_keypair` helper with a different info constant.
```

```
    33→import { useVaultStore } from '../stores/vault.store';
    34→import { useQuotaStore } from '../stores/quota.store';
    35→import type { FolderNode } from '../stores/folder.store';
    36→
    37→// Track whether TEE enrollment has been done for this session
    38→let binTeeEnrolled = false;
    39→
    40→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    41→// Internal helpers: load / save bin metadata
    42→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    43→
    44→/**
    45→ * Load and decrypt bin metadata from IPNS.
    46→ *
    47→ * @returns Decrypted metadata and IPNS resolution data, or null if no bin exists yet
    48→ */
    49→async function loadBinMetadata(params: { userPrivateKey: Uint8Array }): Promise<{
    50→  metadata: RecycleBinMetadata;
    51→  ipnsName: string;
    52→  sequenceNumber: bigint;
    53→} | null> {
    54→  const binIpns = await deriveBinIpnsKeypair(params.userPrivateKey);
    55→  const resolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(binIpns.ipnsName);
    56→
    57→  if (!resolved) {
    58→    return null;
    59→  }
    60→
    61→  const encryptedBytes = await fetchFromIpfs(resolved.cid);
    62→  const metadata = await decryptBinMetadata(encryptedBytes, params.userPrivateKey);
    63→
    64→  return {
    65→    metadata,
    66→    ipnsName: binIpns.ipnsName,
    67→    sequenceNumber: resolved.sequenceNumber,
    68→  };
    69→}
    70→
    71→/**
    72→ * Encrypt and publish bin metadata to IPNS.
    73→ *
    74→ * Handles TEE enrollment on first write.
    75→ */
    76→async function saveBinMetadata(params: {
    77→  metadata: RecycleBinMetadata;
    78→  userPublicKey: Uint8Array;
    79→  userPrivateKey: Uint8Array;
    80→}): Promise<void> {
    81→  const binIpns = await deriveBinIpnsKeypair(params.userPrivateKey);
    82→
    83→  // Encrypt metadata with user's public key (ECIES)
    84→  const encryptedBytes = await encryptBinMetadata(params.metadata, params.userPublicKey);
    85→
    86→  // Pin to IPFS
    87→  const { cid } = await addToIpfs(new Blob([encryptedBytes as BlobPart]));
    88→
    89→  // TEE enrollment on first write (fire-and-forget)
    90→  let encryptedIpnsKey: string | undefined;
    91→  let keyEpoch: number | undefined;
    92→
    93→  if (!binTeeEnrolled) {
    94→    const teeKeys = useAuthStore.getState().teeKeys;
    95→    if (teeKeys?.currentPublicKey) {
    96→      try {
    97→        const wrappedKey = await wrapKey(binIpns.privateKey, hexToBytes(teeKeys.currentPublicKey));
    98→        encryptedIpnsKey = bytesToHex(wrappedKey);
    99→        keyEpoch = teeKeys.currentEpoch;
   100→        binTeeEnrolled = true;
   101→      } catch (err) {
   102→        console.error('[Bin] TEE enrollment failed (non-blocking):', err);
   103→      }
   104→    }
   105→  }
   106→
   107→  // Publish IPNS record
   108→  await createAndPublishIpnsRecord({
   109→    ipnsPrivateKey: binIpns.privateKey,
   110→    ipnsName: binIpns.ipnsName,
   111→    metadataCid: cid,
   112→    sequenceNumber: BigInt(params.metadata.sequenceNumber),
   113→    encryptedIpnsPrivateKey: encryptedIpnsKey,
   114→    keyEpoch,
   115→  });
   116→}
   117→
   118→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   119→// Public API: initializeBin
   120→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   121→
   122→/**
   123→ * Initialize or load the recycle bin on login.
   124→ *
   125→ * Derives the deterministic IPNS keypair, resolves existing bin metadata
   126→ * from IPNS (or creates empty state), and populates the bin store.
   127→ *
   128→ * Non-blocking: errors are logged, not thrown.
   129→ */
   130→export async function initializeBin(params: {
   131→  userPrivateKey: Uint8Array;
   132→  userPublicKey: Uint8Array;
   133→}): Promise<void> {
   134→  const store = useBinStore.getState();
   135→  store.setLoading(true);
   136→
   137→  try {
   138→    // Reset TEE enrollment tracking for new session
   139→    binTeeEnrolled = false;
   140→
   141→    const binIpns = await deriveBinIpnsKeypair(params.userPrivateKey);
   142→    store.setBinIpnsName(binIpns.ipnsName);
   143→
   144→    // Try to load existing bin metadata
   145→    const loaded = await loadBinMetadata({ userPrivateKey: params.userPrivateKey });
   146→
   147→    if (loaded) {
   148→      store.setEntries(loaded.metadata.entries, loaded.metadata.sequenceNumber);
   149→    } else {
   150→      // No bin exists yet -- initialize with empty state
   151→      store.setEntries([], 0);
   152→    }
   153→  } catch (error) {
   154→    console.error('[Bin] Failed to initialize bin:', error);
   155→    store.setError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Failed to initialize bin');
   156→    store.setLoading(false);
   157→  }
   158→}
   159→
   160→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   161→// Public API: addToBin
   162→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   163→
   164→/**
   165→ * Add a deleted item to the recycle bin.
   166→ *
   167→ * Creates a BinEntry from the FolderChild that was removed from its parent,
   168→ * appends it to the bin metadata, encrypts, and publishes.
   169→ *
   170→ * @param params.item - The FolderChild (FilePointer or FolderEntry) that was removed
   171→ * @param params.parentIpnsName - IPNS name of the original parent folder
   172→ * @param params.parentPath - Breadcrumb path at deletion time (e.g., "My Vault / Documents")
   173→ * @param params.userPublicKey - User's secp256k1 public key for ECIES encryption
   174→ * @param params.userPrivateKey - User's secp256k1 private key for IPNS derivation
   175→ */
   176→export async function addToBin(params: {
   177→  item: FolderChild;
   178→  parentIpnsName: string;
   179→  parentPath: string;
   180→  userPublicKey: Uint8Array;
   181→  userPrivateKey: Uint8Array;
   182→}): Promise<void> {
   183→  const { item, parentIpnsName, parentPath, userPublicKey, userPrivateKey } = params;
   184→
   185→  // Build bin entry
   186→  const isFile = item.type === 'file';
   187→  const entry: BinEntry = {
   188→    id: crypto.randomUUID(),
   189→    itemType: isFile ? 'file' : 'folder',
   190→    name: item.name,
   191→    originalParentIpnsName: parentIpnsName,
   192→    originalPath: parentPath,
   193→    deletedAt: Date.now(),
   194→    size: 0, // Files: resolved lazily on permanent delete. Folders: 0.
   195→    mimeType: '',
   196→    filePointer: isFile ? (item as FilePointer) : undefined,
   197→    folderEntry: !isFile ? (item as FolderEntry) : undefined,
   198→  };
   199→
   200→  // Read current bin state and build updated metadata
   201→  const store = useBinStore.getState();
   202→  const currentEntries = [...store.entries, entry];
   203→  const newSeq = store.sequenceNumber + 1;
   204→
   205→  const metadata: RecycleBinMetadata = {
   206→    version: 'v1',
   207→    sequenceNumber: newSeq,
   208→    entries: currentEntries,
   209→  };
   210→
   211→  // Encrypt and publish
   212→  await saveBinMetadata({ metadata, userPublicKey, userPrivateKey });
   213→
   214→  // Update local store
   215→  store.addEntry(entry);
   216→}
   217→
   218→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   219→// Public API: restoreFromBin
   220→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   221→
   222→/**
   223→ * Restore a bin entry to its original folder.
   224→ *
   225→ * Looks up the target folder by IPNS name. If found, re-adds the preserved
   226→ * FolderChild. Handles name collisions (appends " (restored)"). If original
   227→ * parent is also in the bin, attempts recursive parent restore (max depth 5).
   228→ * Falls back to root folder if parent is gone.
   229→ */
   230→export async function restoreFromBin(params: {
   231→  entryId: string;
   232→  userPublicKey: Uint8Array;
   233→  userPrivateKey: Uint8Array;
   234→}): Promise<void> {
   235→  const { entryId, userPublicKey, userPrivateKey } = params;
   236→
   237→  const binStore = useBinStore.getState();
   238→  const entry = binStore.entries.find((e) => e.id === entryId);
   239→  if (!entry) throw new Error('Bin entry not found');
   240→
   241→  // Resolve original parent folder
   242→  const targetFolder = await resolveTargetFolder(entry.originalParentIpnsName, 0, {
   243→    userPublicKey,
   244→    userPrivateKey,
   245→  });
   246→
   247→  // Get the FolderChild to restore
   248→  const child = getRestoredChild(entry);
   249→
   250→  // Check for name collisions in target folder and rename if needed
   251→  const finalChild = resolveNameCollision(child, targetFolder.children);
   252→
   253→  // Add child back to target folder
   254→  const { updateFolderMetadata } = await import('./folder.service');
   255→  const updatedChildren = [...targetFolder.children, finalChild];
   256→  const { newSequenceNumber } = await updateFolderMetadata({
   257→    folderId: targetFolder.id,
   258→    children: updatedChildren,
   259→    folderKey: targetFolder.folderKey,
   260→    ipnsPrivateKey: targetFolder.ipnsPrivateKey,
   261→    ipnsName: targetFolder.ipnsName,
   262→    sequenceNumber: targetFolder.sequenceNumber,
   263→  });
   264→
   265→  // Update folder store
   266→  const folderStore = useFolderStore.getState();
   267→  folderStore.updateFolderChildren(targetFolder.id, updatedChildren);
   268→  folderStore.updateFolderSequence(targetFolder.id, newSequenceNumber);
   269→
   270→  // If restoring a folder, re-add the folder node to the store
   271→  if (entry.itemType === 'folder' && entry.folderEntry) {
   272→    const existingNode = folderStore.folders[entry.folderEntry.id];
   273→    if (!existingNode) {
   274→      // Create a placeholder node -- it will be fully loaded on next navigation
   275→      folderStore.setFolder({
   276→        id: entry.folderEntry.id,
   277→        name: finalChild.name,
   278→        ipnsName: entry.folderEntry.ipnsName,
   279→        parentId: targetFolder.id,
   280→        children: [],
   281→        isLoaded: false,
   282→        isLoading: false,
   283→        sequenceNumber: 0n,
   284→        // These will be populated when the folder is loaded
   285→        folderKey: new Uint8Array(32),
   286→        ipnsPrivateKey: new Uint8Array(64),
   287→      });
   288→    }
   289→  }
   290→
   291→  // Remove entry from bin metadata and publish
   292→  await removeEntriesFromBin([entryId], { userPublicKey, userPrivateKey });
   293→}
   294→
   295→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   296→// Public API: permanentlyDelete
   297→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   298→
   299→/**
   300→ * Permanently delete a bin entry: unpin CIDs and update quota.
   301→ *
   302→ * For files: resolves file IPNS -> gets CID -> unpins.
   303→ * For folders: recursively resolves all descendant file CIDs -> unpins all.
   304→ */
   305→export async function permanentlyDelete(params: {
   306→  entryId: string;
   307→  userPublicKey: Uint8Array;
   308→  userPrivateKey: Uint8Array;
   309→}): Promise<void> {
   310→  const { entryId, userPublicKey, userPrivateKey } = params;
   311→
   312→  const binStore = useBinStore.getState();
   313→  const entry = binStore.entries.find((e) => e.id === entryId);
   314→  if (!entry) throw new Error('Bin entry not found');
   315→
   316→  // Perform CID cleanup
   317→  await cleanupEntryCids(entry);
   318→
   319→  // Remove from bin metadata and publish
   320→  await removeEntriesFromBin([entryId], { userPublicKey, userPrivateKey });
   321→}
   322→
   323→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   324→// Public API: emptyBin
   325→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   326→
   327→/**
   328→ * Permanently delete all bin entries.
   329→ *
   330→ * Processes each entry's CID cleanup, then publishes empty bin metadata.
   331→ */
   332→export async function emptyBin(params: {
   333→  userPublicKey: Uint8Array;
   334→  userPrivateKey: Uint8Array;
   335→}): Promise<void> {
   336→  const { userPublicKey, userPrivateKey } = params;
   337→
   338→  const binStore = useBinStore.getState();
   339→  const entries = [...binStore.entries];
   340→
   341→  // Cleanup CIDs for all entries (best-effort, continue on individual failures)
   342→  for (const entry of entries) {
   343→    try {
   344→      await cleanupEntryCids(entry);
   345→    } catch (err) {
   346→      console.error(`[Bin] Failed to cleanup CIDs for ${entry.name}:`, err);
   347→    }
   348→  }
   349→
   350→  // Publish empty bin metadata
   351→  const newSeq = binStore.sequenceNumber + 1;
   352→  const metadata: RecycleBinMetadata = {
   353→    version: 'v1',
   354→    sequenceNumber: newSeq,
   355→    entries: [],
   356→  };
   357→
   358→  await saveBinMetadata({ metadata, userPublicKey, userPrivateKey });
   359→
   360→  // Update local store
   361→  binStore.setEntries([], newSeq);
   362→}
   363→
   364→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   365→// Public API: purgeExpired
   366→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   367→
   368→/**
   369→ * Purge expired bin entries based on retention period.
   370→ *
   371→ * Filters entries past their retention, cleans up CIDs, and publishes
   372→ * updated bin metadata in a single IPNS publish.
   373→ */
   374→export async function purgeExpired(params: {
   375→  retentionDays: number;
   376→  userPublicKey: Uint8Array;
   377→  userPrivateKey: Uint8Array;
   378→}): Promise<void> {
   379→  const { retentionDays, userPublicKey, userPrivateKey } = params;
   380→
   381→  const binStore = useBinStore.getState();
   382→  const retentionMs = retentionDays * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
   383→  const now = Date.now();
   384→
   385→  const expired = binStore.entries.filter((e) => now - e.deletedAt > retentionMs);
   386→  if (expired.length === 0) return;
   387→
   388→  console.log(`[Bin] Purging ${expired.length} expired entries`);
   389→
   390→  // Cleanup CIDs for expired entries (best-effort)
   391→  for (const entry of expired) {
   392→    try {
   393→      await cleanupEntryCids(entry);
   394→    } catch (err) {
   395→      console.error(`[Bin] Failed to cleanup CIDs for expired ${entry.name}:`, err);
   396→    }
   397→  }
   398→
   399→  // Remove expired entries and publish
   400→  const expiredIds = expired.map((e) => e.id);
   401→  await removeEntriesFromBin(expiredIds, { userPublicKey, userPrivateKey });
   402→}
   403→
   404→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   405→// Internal helpers
   406→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   407→
   408→/**
   409→ * Remove entries from bin metadata and publish the updated metadata.
   410→ */
   411→async function removeEntriesFromBin(
   412→  entryIds: string[],
   413→  params: { userPublicKey: Uint8Array; userPrivateKey: Uint8Array }
   414→): Promise<void> {
   415→  const binStore = useBinStore.getState();
   416→  const idsToRemove = new Set(entryIds);
   417→  const remainingEntries = binStore.entries.filter((e) => !idsToRemove.has(e.id));
   418→  const newSeq = binStore.sequenceNumber + 1;
   419→
   420→  const metadata: RecycleBinMetadata = {
   421→    version: 'v1',
   422→    sequenceNumber: newSeq,
   423→    entries: remainingEntries,
   424→  };
   425→
   426→  await saveBinMetadata({
   427→    metadata,
   428→    userPublicKey: params.userPublicKey,
   429→    userPrivateKey: params.userPrivateKey,
   430→  });
   431→
   432→  // Update local store
   433→  binStore.removeEntries(entryIds);
   434→}
   435→
   436→/**
   437→ * Cleanup CIDs for a bin entry (unpin from IPFS + update quota).
   438→ *
   439→ * Files: resolve file IPNS -> get CID -> unpin
   440→ * Folders: recursively resolve descendant file CIDs -> unpin all
   441→ */
   442→async function cleanupEntryCids(entry: BinEntry): Promise<void> {
   443→  if (entry.itemType === 'file' && entry.filePointer) {
   444→    const fileIpnsName = entry.filePointer.fileMetaIpnsName;
   445→    if (fileIpnsName) {
   446→      try {
   447→        const resolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(fileIpnsName);
   448→        if (resolved?.cid) {
   449→          // Fetch file metadata to get the actual content CID and size
   450→          const metaBytes = await fetchFromIpfs(resolved.cid);
   451→          const metaJson = new TextDecoder().decode(metaBytes);
   452→          const fileMeta = JSON.parse(metaJson);
   453→
   454→          // Unpin the content CID (encrypted file data)
   455→          if (fileMeta.cid) {
   456→            await unpinFromIpfs(fileMeta.cid);
   457→            // Update quota
   458→            if (fileMeta.size && typeof fileMeta.size === 'number') {
   459→              useQuotaStore.getState().removeUsage(fileMeta.size);
   460→            }
   461→          }
   462→
   463→          // Also unpin the metadata CID
   464→          await unpinFromIpfs(resolved.cid).catch(() => {});
   465→        }
   466→      } catch (err) {
   467→        console.error(`[Bin] CID cleanup failed for file ${entry.name}:`, err);
   468→      }
   469→    }
   470→  } else if (entry.itemType === 'folder' && entry.folderEntry) {
   471→    // Recursively resolve folder to find all descendant file CIDs
   472→    await cleanupFolderCids(entry.folderEntry.ipnsName);
   473→  }
   474→}
   475→
   476→/**
   477→ * Recursively unpin all CIDs in a folder tree.
   478→ */
   479→async function cleanupFolderCids(folderIpnsName: string): Promise<void> {
   480→  try {
   481→    const resolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(folderIpnsName);
   482→    if (!resolved?.cid) return;
   483→
   484→    // Try to load folder metadata -- it may be AES-GCM encrypted
   485→    // We don't have the folder key here, so we check if the folder is
   486→    // loaded in the store first
   487→    const folders = useFolderStore.getState().folders;
   488→    const folderNode = Object.values(folders).find((f) => f.ipnsName === folderIpnsName);
   489→
   490→    if (folderNode) {
   491→      // Folder is loaded -- iterate children
   492→      for (const child of folderNode.children) {
   493→        if (child.type === 'file') {
   494→          const fp = child as FilePointer;
   495→          if (fp.fileMetaIpnsName) {
   496→            try {
   497→              const fileResolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(fp.fileMetaIpnsName);
   498→              if (fileResolved?.cid) {
   499→                const metaBytes = await fetchFromIpfs(fileResolved.cid);
   500→                const metaJson = new TextDecoder().decode(metaBytes);
   501→                const fileMeta = JSON.parse(metaJson);
   502→                if (fileMeta.cid) {
   503→                  await unpinFromIpfs(fileMeta.cid);
   504→                  if (fileMeta.size && typeof fileMeta.size === 'number') {
   505→                    useQuotaStore.getState().removeUsage(fileMeta.size);
   506→                  }
   507→                }
   508→                await unpinFromIpfs(fileResolved.cid).catch(() => {});
   509→              }
   510→            } catch (err) {
   511→              console.error(`[Bin] CID cleanup failed for nested file:`, err);
   512→            }
   513→          }
   514→        } else if (child.type === 'folder') {
   515→          const fe = child as FolderEntry;
   516→          await cleanupFolderCids(fe.ipnsName);
   517→        }
   518→      }
   519→    }
   520→
   521→    // Unpin the folder metadata CID itself
   522→    await unpinFromIpfs(resolved.cid).catch(() => {});
   523→  } catch (err) {
   524→    console.error(`[Bin] Folder CID cleanup failed for ${folderIpnsName}:`, err);
   525→  }
   526→}
   527→
   528→/**
   529→ * Resolve the target folder for a restore operation.
   530→ *
   531→ * Walks the folder store to find a folder with the given IPNS name.
   532→ * If not found, checks if the parent is in the bin (recursive restore).
   533→ * Falls back to root folder.
   534→ */
   535→async function resolveTargetFolder(
   536→  parentIpnsName: string,
   537→  depth: number,
   538→  params: { userPublicKey: Uint8Array; userPrivateKey: Uint8Array }
   539→): Promise<FolderNode> {
   540→  // Max recursion depth to prevent infinite loops
   541→  if (depth > 5) {
   542→    console.warn('[Bin] Max restore depth reached, falling back to root');
   543→    return getRootFolder();
   544→  }
   545→
   546→  // Look up folder by IPNS name in folder store
   547→  const folders = useFolderStore.getState().folders;
   548→  const targetFolder = Object.values(folders).find((f) => f.ipnsName === parentIpnsName);
   549→
   550→  if (targetFolder) {
   551→    return targetFolder;
   552→  }
   553→
   554→  // Check if parent is in the bin
   555→  const binStore = useBinStore.getState();
   556→  const parentBinEntry = binStore.entries.find(
   557→    (e) => e.itemType === 'folder' && e.folderEntry && e.folderEntry.ipnsName === parentIpnsName
   558→  );
   559→
   560→  if (parentBinEntry) {
   561→    // Restore parent first (recursive)
   562→    console.log(`[Bin] Restoring parent folder "${parentBinEntry.name}" first`);
   563→    await restoreFromBin({
   564→      entryId: parentBinEntry.id,
   565→      userPublicKey: params.userPublicKey,
   566→      userPrivateKey: params.userPrivateKey,
   567→    });
   568→
   569→    // After restoring parent, try to find it again
   570→    const updatedFolders = useFolderStore.getState().folders;
   571→    const restoredParent = Object.values(updatedFolders).find((f) => f.ipnsName === parentIpnsName);
   572→    if (restoredParent) {
   573→      return restoredParent;
   574→    }
   575→  }
   576→
   577→  // Parent truly gone -- restore to root
   578→  console.warn(`[Bin] Original parent not found (${parentIpnsName}), restoring to root`);
   579→  return getRootFolder();
   580→}
   581→
   582→/**
   583→ * Get the root folder from the folder store.
   584→ */
   585→function getRootFolder(): FolderNode {
   586→  const folders = useFolderStore.getState().folders;
   587→  const root = folders['root'];
   588→  if (root) return root;
   589→
   590→  // Fallback: construct from vault store
   591→  const vault = useVaultStore.getState();
   592→  if (!vault.rootFolderKey || !vault.rootIpnsKeypair || !vault.rootIpnsName) {
   593→    throw new Error('Vault not initialized - cannot restore to root');
   594→  }
   595→
   596→  return {
   597→    id: 'root',
   598→    name: 'My Vault',
   599→    ipnsName: vault.rootIpnsName,
   600→    parentId: null,
   601→    children: folders['root']?.children ?? [],
   602→    isLoaded: true,
   603→    isLoading: false,
   604→    sequenceNumber: 0n,
   605→    folderKey: vault.rootFolderKey,
   606→    ipnsPrivateKey: vault.rootIpnsKeypair.privateKey,
   607→  };
   608→}
   609→
   610→/**
   611→ * Get the FolderChild to restore from a bin entry.
   612→ */
   613→function getRestoredChild(entry: BinEntry): FolderChild {
   614→  if (entry.itemType === 'file' && entry.filePointer) {
   615→    return { ...entry.filePointer, modifiedAt: Date.now() };
   616→  } else if (entry.itemType === 'folder' && entry.folderEntry) {
   617→    return { ...entry.folderEntry, modifiedAt: Date.now() };
   618→  }
   619→  throw new Error(`Bin entry ${entry.id} has no stored item reference`);
   620→}
   621→
   622→/**
   623→ * Check for name collisions and rename if needed.
   624→ */
   625→function resolveNameCollision(child: FolderChild, existingChildren: FolderChild[]): FolderChild {
   626→  const existingNames = new Set(existingChildren.map((c) => c.name));
   627→  if (!existingNames.has(child.name)) {
   628→    return child;
   629→  }
   630→
   631→  // Append " (restored)" suffix, incrementing if needed
   632→  let newName = `${child.name} (restored)`;
   633→  let counter = 2;
   634→  while (existingNames.has(newName)) {
   635→    newName = `${child.name} (restored ${counter})`;
   636→    counter++;
   637→  }
   638→
   639→  return { ...child, name: newName };
   640→}
   641→
```

```
     1→import { create } from 'zustand';
     2→import type { BinEntry } from '@cipherbox/crypto';
     3→
     4→type BinState = {
     5→  /** Current bin entries */
     6→  entries: BinEntry[];
     7→  /** True while loading bin metadata from IPNS */
     8→  isLoading: boolean;
     9→  /** True after first successful load */
    10→  isLoaded: boolean;
    11→  /** Last error message, null = no error */
    12→  error: string | null;
    13→  /** Monotonically increasing update counter (metadata-level, not IPNS-level) */
    14→  sequenceNumber: number;
    15→  /** IPNS name for the bin record (display/debug) */
    16→  binIpnsName: string | null;
    17→  /** Retention period from API config (days) */
    18→  retentionDays: number;
    19→
    20→  // Actions
    21→  setEntries: (entries: BinEntry[], seq: number) => void;
    22→  addEntry: (entry: BinEntry) => void;
    23→  removeEntry: (entryId: string) => void;
    24→  removeEntries: (entryIds: string[]) => void;
    25→  setLoading: (loading: boolean) => void;
    26→  setLoaded: (loaded: boolean) => void;
    27→  setError: (error: string | null) => void;
    28→  setBinIpnsName: (name: string) => void;
    29→  setRetentionDays: (days: number) => void;
    30→  clearBin: () => void;
    31→};
    32→
    33→/**
    34→ * Bin store for managing recycle bin state.
    35→ *
    36→ * Tracks soft-deleted items with their metadata, loading state,
    37→ * and IPNS name. Cleared on logout for clean lifecycle.
    38→ *
    39→ * Used by:
    40→ * - useAuth (bin initialization after login, clear on logout)
    41→ * - useBin (CRUD operations, auto-purge)
    42→ * - useFolderMutations (addToBin on delete)
    43→ */
    44→export const useBinStore = create<BinState>((set) => ({
    45→  // State
    46→  entries: [],
    47→  isLoading: false,
    48→  isLoaded: false,
    49→  error: null,
    50→  sequenceNumber: 0,
    51→  binIpnsName: null,
    52→  retentionDays: 30,
    53→
    54→  // Actions
    55→  setEntries: (entries, seq) =>
    56→    set({
    57→      entries,
    58→      sequenceNumber: seq,
    59→      isLoaded: true,
    60→      isLoading: false,
    61→      error: null,
    62→    }),
    63→
    64→  addEntry: (entry) =>
    65→    set((state) => ({
    66→      entries: [...state.entries, entry],
    67→      sequenceNumber: state.sequenceNumber + 1,
    68→    })),
    69→
    70→  removeEntry: (entryId) =>
    71→    set((state) => ({
    72→      entries: state.entries.filter((e) => e.id !== entryId),
    73→      sequenceNumber: state.sequenceNumber + 1,
    74→    })),
    75→
    76→  removeEntries: (entryIds) =>
    77→    set((state) => {
    78→      const ids = new Set(entryIds);
    79→      return {
    80→        entries: state.entries.filter((e) => !ids.has(e.id)),
    81→        sequenceNumber: state.sequenceNumber + 1,
    82→      };
    83→    }),
    84→
    85→  setLoading: (loading) => set({ isLoading: loading }),
    86→
    87→  setLoaded: (loaded) => set({ isLoaded: loaded }),
    88→
    89→  setError: (error) => set({ error }),
    90→
    91→  setBinIpnsName: (name) => set({ binIpnsName: name }),
    92→
    93→  setRetentionDays: (days) =>
    94→    set({
    95→      retentionDays: Number.isFinite(days) ? Math.max(1, Math.floor(days)) : 30,
    96→    }),
    97→
    98→  clearBin: () =>
    99→    set({
   100→      entries: [],
   101→      isLoading: false,
   102→      isLoaded: false,
   103→      error: null,
   104→      sequenceNumber: 0,
   105→      binIpnsName: null,
   106→      retentionDays: 30,
   107→    }),
   108→}));
   109→
```

```
     1→import { useState, useCallback } from 'react';
     2→import { useBinStore } from '../stores/bin.store';
     3→import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth.store';
     4→import {
     5→  initializeBin,
     6→  restoreFromBin,
     7→  permanentlyDelete,
     8→  emptyBin,
     9→  purgeExpired,
    10→} from '../services/bin.service';
    11→import type { BinEntry } from '@cipherbox/crypto';
    12→
    13→/**
    14→ * React hook for recycle bin operations.
    15→ *
    16→ * Wraps bin service functions with loading/error state management.
    17→ * Provides single-item and batch operations for restore and permanent delete.
    18→ */
    19→export function useBin() {
    20→  const [state, setState] = useState<{ isLoading: boolean; error: string | null }>({
    21→    isLoading: false,
    22→    error: null,
    23→  });
    24→
    25→  const entries = useBinStore((s) => s.entries);
    26→  const isLoaded = useBinStore((s) => s.isLoaded);
    27→  const retentionDays = useBinStore((s) => s.retentionDays);
    28→
    29→  /**
    30→   * Load bin metadata from IPNS and trigger auto-purge of expired entries.
    31→   */
    32→  const loadBin = useCallback(async () => {
    33→    const auth = useAuthStore.getState();
    34→    if (!auth.vaultKeypair) return;
    35→
    36→    setState({ isLoading: true, error: null });
    37→    try {
    38→      await initializeBin({
    39→        userPrivateKey: auth.vaultKeypair.privateKey,
    40→        userPublicKey: auth.vaultKeypair.publicKey,
    41→      });
    42→
    43→      // Non-blocking: purge expired entries after loading
    44→      const currentRetention = useBinStore.getState().retentionDays;
    45→      void purgeExpired({
    46→        retentionDays: currentRetention,
    47→        userPublicKey: auth.vaultKeypair.publicKey,
    48→        userPrivateKey: auth.vaultKeypair.privateKey,
    49→      }).catch(() => {
    50→        console.error('[useBin] Auto-purge failed (non-blocking)');
    51→      });
    52→
    53→      setState({ isLoading: false, error: null });
    54→    } catch (err) {
    55→      const error = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to load bin';
    56→      setState({ isLoading: false, error });
    57→    }
    58→  }, []);
    59→
    60→  /**
    61→   * Restore a single bin entry to its original folder.
    62→   */
    63→  const restore = useCallback(async (entryId: string) => {
    64→    const auth = useAuthStore.getState();
    65→    if (!auth.vaultKeypair) throw new Error('Not authenticated');
    66→
    67→    setState({ isLoading: true, error: null });
    68→    try {
    69→      await restoreFromBin({
    70→        entryId,
    71→        userPublicKey: auth.vaultKeypair.publicKey,
    72→        userPrivateKey: auth.vaultKeypair.privateKey,
    73→      });
    74→      setState({ isLoading: false, error: null });
    75→    } catch (err) {
    76→      const error = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to restore item';
    77→      setState({ isLoading: false, error });
    78→      throw err;
    79→    }
    80→  }, []);
    81→
    82→  /**
    83→   * Permanently delete a single bin entry (unpin CIDs, update quota).
    84→   */
    85→  const permanentDelete = useCallback(async (entryId: string) => {
    86→    const auth = useAuthStore.getState();
    87→    if (!auth.vaultKeypair) throw new Error('Not authenticated');
    88→
    89→    setState({ isLoading: true, error: null });
    90→    try {
    91→      await permanentlyDelete({
    92→        entryId,
    93→        userPublicKey: auth.vaultKeypair.publicKey,
    94→        userPrivateKey: auth.vaultKeypair.privateKey,
    95→      });
    96→      setState({ isLoading: false, error: null });
    97→    } catch (err) {
    98→      const error = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to permanently delete';
    99→      setState({ isLoading: false, error });
   100→      throw err;
   101→    }
   102→  }, []);
   103→
   104→  /**
   105→   * Empty all bin entries permanently.
   106→   */
   107→  const emptyAll = useCallback(async () => {
   108→    const auth = useAuthStore.getState();
   109→    if (!auth.vaultKeypair) throw new Error('Not authenticated');
   110→
   111→    setState({ isLoading: true, error: null });
   112→    try {
   113→      await emptyBin({
   114→        userPublicKey: auth.vaultKeypair.publicKey,
   115→        userPrivateKey: auth.vaultKeypair.privateKey,
   116→      });
   117→      setState({ isLoading: false, error: null });
   118→    } catch (err) {
   119→      const error = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to empty bin';
   120→      setState({ isLoading: false, error });
   121→      throw err;
   122→    }
   123→  }, []);
   124→
   125→  /**
   126→   * Restore multiple bin entries.
   127→   */
   128→  const restoreMultiple = useCallback(async (entryIds: string[]) => {
   129→    const auth = useAuthStore.getState();
   130→    if (!auth.vaultKeypair) throw new Error('Not authenticated');
   131→
   132→    setState({ isLoading: true, error: null });
   133→    try {
   134→      for (const entryId of entryIds) {
   135→        await restoreFromBin({
   136→          entryId,
   137→          userPublicKey: auth.vaultKeypair.publicKey,
   138→          userPrivateKey: auth.vaultKeypair.privateKey,
   139→        });
   140→      }
   141→      setState({ isLoading: false, error: null });
   142→    } catch (err) {
   143→      const error = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to restore items';
   144→      setState({ isLoading: false, error });
   145→      throw err;
   146→    }
   147→  }, []);
   148→
   149→  /**
   150→   * Permanently delete multiple bin entries.
   151→   */
   152→  const permanentDeleteMultiple = useCallback(async (entryIds: string[]) => {
   153→    const auth = useAuthStore.getState();
   154→    if (!auth.vaultKeypair) throw new Error('Not authenticated');
   155→
   156→    setState({ isLoading: true, error: null });
   157→    try {
   158→      for (const entryId of entryIds) {
   159→        await permanentlyDelete({
   160→          entryId,
   161→          userPublicKey: auth.vaultKeypair.publicKey,
   162→          userPrivateKey: auth.vaultKeypair.privateKey,
   163→        });
   164→      }
   165→      setState({ isLoading: false, error: null });
   166→    } catch (err) {
   167→      const error = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to permanently delete items';
   168→      setState({ isLoading: false, error });
   169→      throw err;
   170→    }
   171→  }, []);
   172→
   173→  /**
   174→   * Calculate days remaining before a bin entry is auto-purged.
   175→   */
   176→  const daysRemaining = useCallback(
   177→    (entry: BinEntry): number => {
   178→      const elapsed = Date.now() - entry.deletedAt;
   179→      const retentionMs = retentionDays * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
   180→      return Math.max(0, Math.ceil((retentionMs - elapsed) / (24 * 60 * 60 * 1000)));
   181→    },
   182→    [retentionDays]
   183→  );
   184→
   185→  return {
   186→    ...state,
   187→    entries,
   188→    isLoaded,
   189→    retentionDays,
   190→    daysRemaining,
   191→    loadBin,
   192→    restore,
   193→    permanentDelete,
   194→    emptyAll,
   195→    restoreMultiple,
   196→    permanentDeleteMultiple,
   197→  };
   198→}
   199→
```

```
     1→import { useCallback, type MouseEvent } from 'react';
     2→import type { BinEntry } from '@cipherbox/crypto';
     3→import { formatBytes } from '../../utils/format';
     4→
     5→type BinListItemProps = {
     6→  /** The bin entry to display */
     7→  entry: BinEntry;
     8→  /** Whether this item is currently selected */
     9→  isSelected: boolean;
    10→  /** Days remaining before auto-purge */
    11→  daysLeft: number;
    12→  /** Callback when item is clicked (with modifier key info) */
    13→  onSelect: (
    14→    entryId: string,
    15→    event: { ctrlKey: boolean; shiftKey: boolean; metaKey: boolean }
    16→  ) => void;
    17→  /** Callback when right-click context menu is requested */
    18→  onContextMenu: (event: MouseEvent, entry: BinEntry) => void;
    19→};
    20→
    21→/**
    22→ * Format a deletion timestamp as a relative time string.
    23→ */
    24→function formatRelativeTime(timestamp: number): string {
    25→  const diff = Date.now() - timestamp;
    26→  const seconds = Math.floor(diff / 1000);
    27→  const minutes = Math.floor(seconds / 60);
    28→  const hours = Math.floor(minutes / 60);
    29→  const days = Math.floor(hours / 24);
    30→
    31→  if (seconds < 60) return 'just now';
    32→  if (minutes < 60) return `${minutes}m ago`;
    33→  if (hours < 24) return `${hours}h ago`;
    34→  if (days === 1) return '1 day ago';
    35→  return `${days} days ago`;
    36→}
    37→
    38→/**
    39→ * Get a terminal-style type indicator based on item type and MIME.
    40→ */
    41→function getItemIcon(entry: BinEntry): string {
    42→  if (entry.itemType === 'folder') return '[DIR]';
    43→  if (!entry.mimeType) return '[FILE]';
    44→  if (entry.mimeType.startsWith('image/')) return '[IMG]';
    45→  if (entry.mimeType.startsWith('video/')) return '[VID]';
    46→  if (entry.mimeType.startsWith('audio/')) return '[AUD]';
    47→  if (entry.mimeType.startsWith('text/')) return '[TXT]';
    48→  if (entry.mimeType === 'application/pdf') return '[PDF]';
    49→  return '[FILE]';
    50→}
    51→
    52→/**
    53→ * Single row in the bin list.
    54→ *
    55→ * Displays deleted item with name, original location, deletion date,
    56→ * file size, and days remaining before auto-purge.
    57→ */
    58→export function BinListItem({
    59→  entry,
    60→  isSelected,
    61→  daysLeft,
    62→  onSelect,
    63→  onContextMenu,
    64→}: BinListItemProps) {
    65→  const handleClick = useCallback(
    66→    (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    67→      onSelect(entry.id, { ctrlKey: e.ctrlKey, shiftKey: e.shiftKey, metaKey: e.metaKey });
    68→    },
    69→    [entry.id, onSelect]
    70→  );
    71→
    72→  const handleCheckboxClick = useCallback(
    73→    (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    74→      e.stopPropagation();
    75→      onSelect(entry.id, { ctrlKey: true, shiftKey: false, metaKey: false });
    76→    },
    77→    [entry.id, onSelect]
    78→  );
    79→
    80→  const handleContextMenu = useCallback(
    81→    (e: MouseEvent) => {
    82→      e.preventDefault();
    83→      onContextMenu(e, entry);
    84→    },
    85→    [entry, onContextMenu]
    86→  );
    87→
    88→  const sizeDisplay =
    89→    entry.itemType === 'folder' && entry.size === 0 ? 'Folder' : formatBytes(entry.size);
    90→
    91→  const remainingText = daysLeft <= 0 ? 'expired' : daysLeft === 1 ? '< 1 day' : `${daysLeft}d`;
    92→
    93→  const isWarning = daysLeft <= 3;
    94→
    95→  const className = [
    96→    'bin-list-item',
    97→    isSelected ? 'bin-list-item--selected' : '',
    98→    isWarning ? 'bin-list-item--warning' : '',
    99→  ]
   100→    .filter(Boolean)
   101→    .join(' ');
   102→
   103→  return (
   104→    <div
   105→      className={className}
   106→      data-testid={`bin-item-${entry.id}`}
   107→      onClick={handleClick}
   108→      onContextMenu={handleContextMenu}
   109→      role="row"
   110→      aria-selected={isSelected}
   111→      tabIndex={0}
   112→      onKeyDown={(e) => {
   113→        if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {
   114→          e.preventDefault();
   115→          onSelect(entry.id, { ctrlKey: e.ctrlKey, shiftKey: e.shiftKey, metaKey: e.metaKey });
   116→        }
   117→      }}
   118→    >
   119→      <div className="bin-list-item-name" role="gridcell">
   120→        <span
   121→          className="bin-list-item-checkbox"
   122→          onClick={handleCheckboxClick}
   123→          role="checkbox"
   124→          aria-checked={isSelected}
   125→          aria-label={`Select ${entry.name}`}
   126→          tabIndex={0}
   127→          onKeyDown={(e) => {
   128→            if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {
   129→              e.preventDefault();
   130→              e.stopPropagation();
   131→              onSelect(entry.id, { ctrlKey: true, shiftKey: false, metaKey: false });
   132→            }
   133→          }}
   134→        >
   135→          {isSelected ? '[x]' : '[ ]'}
   136→        </span>
   137→        <span className="bin-list-item-icon" aria-hidden="true">
   138→          {getItemIcon(entry)}
   139→        </span>
   140→        <span className="bin-list-item-label">{entry.name}</span>
   141→      </div>
   142→      <div className="bin-list-item-path" role="gridcell" title={entry.originalPath}>
   143→        {entry.originalPath}
   144→      </div>
   145→      <div className="bin-list-item-deleted" role="gridcell">
   146→        {formatRelativeTime(entry.deletedAt)}
   147→      </div>
   148→      <div className="bin-list-item-size" role="gridcell">
   149→        {sizeDisplay}
   150→      </div>
   151→      <div
   152→        className={`bin-list-item-remaining ${isWarning ? 'bin-list-item-remaining--warning' : ''}`}
   153→        role="gridcell"
   154→      >
   155→        {remainingText}
   156→      </div>
   157→    </div>
   158→  );
   159→}
   160→
```
