     1→import {
     2→  useState,
     3→  useCallback,
     4→  useRef,
     5→  type DragEvent,
     6→  type MouseEvent,
     7→  type TouchEvent,
     8→} from 'react';
     9→import type { FolderChild, FilePointer, FolderEntry } from '@cipherbox/crypto';
    10→import { formatBytes, formatDate } from '../../utils/format';
    11→import { useFileSize } from '../../hooks/useFileSize';
    12→import { isExternalFileDrag } from '../../hooks/useDropUpload';
    13→
    14→/**
    15→ * Long press duration in milliseconds for touch context menu.
    16→ */
    17→const LONG_PRESS_DURATION = 500;
    18→
    19→/** Drag payload item (id + type). */
    20→export type DragItem = { id: string; type: 'file' | 'folder' };
    21→
    22→type FileListItemProps = {
    23→  /** The file or folder item to display */
    24→  item: FolderChild;
    25→  /** Whether this item is currently selected */
    26→  isSelected: boolean;
    27→  /** Parent folder ID (for drag data) */
    28→  parentId: string;
    29→  /** All currently selected item IDs (for multi-select drag) */
    30→  selectedIds: Set<string>;
    31→  /** All items in the current folder (for resolving types of selected IDs) */
    32→  allItems: FolderChild[];
    33→  /** Parent folder's decrypted AES-256 key (for resolving file sizes) */
    34→  folderKey: Uint8Array | null;
    35→  /** Callback when item is clicked (with modifier key info) */
    36→  onSelect: (
    37→    itemId: string,
    38→    event: { ctrlKey: boolean; shiftKey: boolean; metaKey: boolean }
    39→  ) => void;
    40→  /** Callback when folder is double-clicked to navigate into */
    41→  onNavigate: (folderId: string) => void;
    42→  /** Callback when right-click context menu is requested */
    43→  onContextMenu: (event: MouseEvent, item: FolderChild) => void;
    44→  /** Callback when drag starts */
    45→  onDragStart: (event: DragEvent, item: FolderChild) => void;
    46→  /** Callback when items are dropped onto this folder (folders only) */
    47→  onDrop?: (items: DragItem[], sourceParentId: string) => void;
    48→  /** Callback when external files are dropped onto this folder */
    49→  onExternalFileDrop?: (files: File[], destFolderId: string) => void;
    50→};
    51→
    52→/**
    53→ * Type guard for folder entries.
    54→ */
    55→function isFolder(item: FolderChild): item is FolderEntry {
    56→  return item.type === 'folder';
    57→}
    58→
    59→/**
    60→ * Type guard for file pointers (v2).
    61→ */
    62→function isFile(item: FolderChild): item is FilePointer {
    63→  return item.type === 'file';
    64→}
    65→
    66→/**
    67→ * Get text prefix for item type (terminal-style).
    68→ */
    69→function getItemIcon(item: FolderChild): string {
    70→  if (isFolder(item)) {
    71→    return '[DIR]';
    72→  }
    73→  return '[FILE]';
    74→}
    75→
    76→/* File extension helper removed - TYPE column no longer used */
    77→
    78→/**
    79→ * Single row in the file list.
    80→ *
    81→ * Displays file/folder with icon, name, size, and date.
    82→ * Supports selection, navigation (for folders), context menu, and drag-drop.
    83→ */
    84→export function FileListItem({
    85→  item,
    86→  isSelected,
    87→  parentId,
    88→  selectedIds,
    89→  allItems,
    90→  folderKey,
    91→  onSelect,
    92→  onNavigate,
    93→  onContextMenu,
    94→  onDragStart,
    95→  onDrop,
    96→  onExternalFileDrop,
    97→}: FileListItemProps) {
    98→  // Ref for long press timer
    99→  const longPressTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
   100→  const touchStartPosRef = useRef<{ x: number; y: number } | null>(null);
   101→
   102→  // Drag-over state for folder drop targets
   103→  const [isDragOver, setIsDragOver] = useState(false);
   104→
   105→  /**
   106→   * Handle single click - select the item.
   107→   * Passes modifier key state for multi-selection support.
   108→   */
   109→  const handleClick = useCallback(
   110→    (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
   111→      onSelect(item.id, { ctrlKey: e.ctrlKey, shiftKey: e.shiftKey, metaKey: e.metaKey });
   112→    },
   113→    [item.id, onSelect]
   114→  );
   115→
   116→  /**
   117→   * Handle checkbox click - toggle selection without modifiers.
   118→   */
   119→  const handleCheckboxClick = useCallback(
   120→    (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
   121→      e.stopPropagation();
   122→      // Checkbox click always toggles like Ctrl+click
   123→      onSelect(item.id, { ctrlKey: true, shiftKey: false, metaKey: false });
   124→    },
   125→    [item.id, onSelect]
   126→  );
   127→
   128→  /**
   129→   * Handle double click - navigate into folder.
   130→   */
   131→  const handleDoubleClick = useCallback(() => {
   132→    if (isFolder(item)) {
   133→      onNavigate(item.id);
   134→    }
   135→  }, [item, onNavigate]);
   136→
   137→  /**
   138→   * Handle context menu (right-click).
   139→   */
   140→  const handleContextMenu = useCallback(
   141→    (e: MouseEvent) => {
   142→      e.preventDefault();
   143→      onContextMenu(e, item);
   144→    },
   145→    [item, onContextMenu]
   146→  );
   147→
   148→  /**
   149→   * Handle mobile action button click.
   150→   * Creates a synthetic mouse event positioned at the button for context menu.
   151→   */
   152→  const handleActionButtonClick = useCallback(
   153→    (e: React.MouseEvent<HTMLButtonElement>) => {
   154→      e.stopPropagation();
   155→      const rect = e.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();
   156→      const syntheticEvent = {
   157→        preventDefault: () => {},
   158→        stopPropagation: () => {},
   159→        clientX: rect.left,
   160→        clientY: rect.bottom,
   161→      } as unknown as MouseEvent;
   162→      onContextMenu(syntheticEvent, item);
   163→    },
   164→    [item, onContextMenu]
   165→  );
   166→
   167→  /**
   168→   * Handle drag start - serialize item data.
   169→   */
   170→  const handleDragStart = useCallback(
   171→    (e: DragEvent) => {
   172→      // Build items array: if dragged item is part of multi-selection, include all selected
   173→      let items: DragItem[];
   174→      if (isSelected && selectedIds.size > 1) {
   175→        items = allItems
   176→          .filter((i) => selectedIds.has(i.id))
   177→          .map((i) => ({ id: i.id, type: i.type }));
   178→      } else {
   179→        items = [{ id: item.id, type: item.type }];
   180→      }
   181→
   182→      e.dataTransfer.setData('application/json', JSON.stringify({ items, parentId }));
   183→      e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move';
   184→      onDragStart(e, item);
   185→    },
   186→    [item, isSelected, selectedIds, allItems, parentId, onDragStart]
   187→  );
   188→
   189→  /**
   190→   * Clear long press timer.
   191→   */
   192→  const clearLongPressTimer = useCallback(() => {
   193→    if (longPressTimerRef.current) {
   194→      clearTimeout(longPressTimerRef.current);
   195→      longPressTimerRef.current = null;
   196→    }
   197→    touchStartPosRef.current = null;
   198→  }, []);
   199→
   200→  /**
   201→   * Handle touch start - begin long press detection.
   202→   */
   203→  const handleTouchStart = useCallback(
   204→    (e: TouchEvent) => {
   205→      const touch = e.touches[0];
   206→      if (!touch) return;
   207→
   208→      // Store touch position for move detection
   209→      touchStartPosRef.current = { x: touch.clientX, y: touch.clientY };
   210→
   211→      // Start long press timer
   212→      longPressTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
   213→        // Create synthetic mouse event for context menu
   214→        const syntheticEvent = {
   215→          preventDefault: () => {},
   216→          stopPropagation: () => {},
   217→          clientX: touch.clientX,
   218→          clientY: touch.clientY,
   219→        } as unknown as MouseEvent;
   220→        onContextMenu(syntheticEvent, item);
   221→        clearLongPressTimer();
   222→      }, LONG_PRESS_DURATION);
   223→    },
   224→    [item, onContextMenu, clearLongPressTimer]
   225→  );
   226→
   227→  /**
   228→   * Handle touch move - cancel long press if moved too far.
   229→   */
   230→  const handleTouchMove = useCallback(
   231→    (e: TouchEvent) => {
   232→      if (!touchStartPosRef.current || !longPressTimerRef.current) return;
   233→
   234→      const touch = e.touches[0];
   235→      if (!touch) return;
   236→
   237→      // Cancel if moved more than 10px
   238→      const dx = Math.abs(touch.clientX - touchStartPosRef.current.x);
   239→      const dy = Math.abs(touch.clientY - touchStartPosRef.current.y);
   240→      if (dx > 10 || dy > 10) {
   241→        clearLongPressTimer();
   242→      }
   243→    },
   244→    [clearLongPressTimer]
   245→  );
   246→
   247→  /**
   248→   * Handle touch end - clear long press timer.
   249→   */
   250→  const handleTouchEnd = useCallback(() => {
   251→    clearLongPressTimer();
   252→  }, [clearLongPressTimer]);
   253→
   254→  /**
   255→   * Handle drag over - allow drop on folders.
   256→   * Accepts both internal drags (move) and external file drags (upload).
   257→   */
   258→  const handleDragOver = useCallback(
   259→    (e: DragEvent) => {
   260→      // Only allow drop on folders
   261→      if (!isFolder(item)) return;
   262→
   263→      e.preventDefault();
   264→      e.stopPropagation();
   265→
   266→      // External files get 'copy' effect, internal moves get 'move'
   267→      e.dataTransfer.dropEffect = isExternalFileDrag(e.dataTransfer) ? 'copy' : 'move';
   268→      setIsDragOver(true);
   269→    },
   270→    [item]
   271→  );
   272→
   273→  /**
   274→   * Handle drag leave - clear drag-over state.
   275→   */
   276→  const handleDragLeave = useCallback((e: DragEvent) => {
   277→    e.preventDefault();
   278→    e.stopPropagation();
   279→    setIsDragOver(false);
   280→  }, []);
   281→
   282→  /**
   283→   * Handle drop - move item to this folder, or upload external files.
   284→   */
   285→  const handleDrop = useCallback(
   286→    (e: DragEvent) => {
   287→      e.preventDefault();
   288→      e.stopPropagation();
   289→      setIsDragOver(false);
   290→
   291→      // Only handle drop on folders
   292→      if (!isFolder(item)) return;
   293→
   294→      // Read JSON once — empty string means external file drag from OS
   295→      const jsonData = e.dataTransfer.getData('application/json');
   296→
   297→      if (!jsonData) {
   298→        // External file drop
   299→        if (e.dataTransfer.files.length > 0 && onExternalFileDrop) {
   300→          const files = Array.from(e.dataTransfer.files);
   301→          onExternalFileDrop(files, item.id);
   302→        }
   303→        return;
   304→      }
   305→
   306→      // Internal move operation
   307→      if (!onDrop) return;
   308→
   309→      try {
   310→        const parsed = JSON.parse(jsonData) as {
   311→          items: DragItem[];
   312→          parentId: string;
   313→        };
   314→
   315→        // Filter out items that are this folder or already in this folder
   316→        const validItems = parsed.items.filter(
   317→          (i) => i.id !== item.id && parsed.parentId !== item.id
   318→        );
   319→        if (validItems.length === 0) return;
   320→
   321→        onDrop(validItems, parsed.parentId);
   322→      } catch {
   323→        // Invalid drag data, ignore
   324→      }
   325→    },
   326→    [item, onDrop, onExternalFileDrop]
   327→  );
   328→
   329→  // Lazily resolve file size from per-file IPNS metadata
   330→  const fileSize = useFileSize(isFile(item) ? item.fileMetaIpnsName : null, folderKey);
   331→
   332→  // Display size: resolved from IPNS metadata for files, dash for folders
   333→  const sizeDisplay = isFile(item) ? (fileSize !== null ? formatBytes(fileSize) : '...') : '-';
   334→
   335→  // Display modified date
   336→  const dateDisplay = formatDate(item.modifiedAt);
   337→
   338→  // Build class name with drag-over state
   339→  const className = [
   340→    'file-list-item',
   341→    isSelected ? 'file-list-item--selected' : '',
   342→    isDragOver && isFolder(item) ? 'file-list-item--drag-over' : '',
   343→  ]
   344→    .filter(Boolean)
   345→    .join(' ');
   346→
   347→  return (
   348→    <div
   349→      className={className}
   350→      data-item-id={item.id}
   351→      onClick={handleClick}
   352→      onDoubleClick={handleDoubleClick}
   353→      onContextMenu={handleContextMenu}
   354→      draggable
   355→      onDragStart={handleDragStart}
   356→      onDragOver={isFolder(item) && (onDrop || onExternalFileDrop) ? handleDragOver : undefined}
   357→      onDragLeave={isFolder(item) && (onDrop || onExternalFileDrop) ? handleDragLeave : undefined}
   358→      onDrop={isFolder(item) && (onDrop || onExternalFileDrop) ? handleDrop : undefined}
   359→      onTouchStart={handleTouchStart}
   360→      onTouchMove={handleTouchMove}
   361→      onTouchEnd={handleTouchEnd}
   362→      onTouchCancel={handleTouchEnd}
   363→      role="row"
   364→      tabIndex={0}
   365→      onKeyDown={(e) => {
   366→        if (e.key === 'Enter') {
   367→          if (isFolder(item)) {
   368→            onNavigate(item.id);
   369→          }
   370→        }
   371→      }}
   372→    >
   373→      {/* Row 1: Checkbox + Icon + Name (for mobile top row) */}
   374→      <div className="file-list-item-row-top" role="gridcell">
   375→        <span
   376→          className="file-list-item-checkbox"
   377→          onClick={handleCheckboxClick}
   378→          role="checkbox"
   379→          aria-checked={isSelected}
   380→          aria-label={`Select ${item.name}`}
   381→          tabIndex={-1}
   382→          onKeyDown={(e) => {
   383→            if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {
   384→              e.preventDefault();
   385→              e.stopPropagation();
   386→              onSelect(item.id, { ctrlKey: true, shiftKey: false, metaKey: false });
   387→            }
   388→          }}
   389→        >
   390→          {isSelected ? '[x]' : '[ ]'}
   391→        </span>
   392→        <span className="file-list-item-icon" aria-hidden="true">
   393→          {getItemIcon(item)}
   394→        </span>
   395→        <span className="file-list-item-name">{item.name}</span>
   396→      </div>
   397→
   398→      {/* Row 2: Size + Date (for mobile bottom row) */}
   399→      <div className="file-list-item-row-bottom">
   400→        <span className="file-list-item-size" role="gridcell">
   401→          {sizeDisplay}
   402→        </span>
   403→        <span className="file-list-item-date" role="gridcell">
   404→          {dateDisplay}
   405→        </span>
   406→      </div>
   407→
   408→      {/* Mobile action button - hidden on desktop via CSS */}
   409→      <div className="file-list-item-mobile-actions" role="gridcell">
   410→        <button
   411→          type="button"
   412→          className="file-list-item-action-btn"
   413→          onClick={handleActionButtonClick}
   414→          aria-label={`Actions for ${item.name}`}
   415→        >
   416→          ...
   417→        </button>
   418→      </div>
   419→    </div>
   420→  );
   421→}
   422→
```

   183→/**
   184→ * Format a Unix ms timestamp as a relative time string.
   185→ */
   186→function formatRelativeTime(timestampMs: number): string {
   187→  const now = Date.now();
   188→  const diff = now - timestampMs;
   189→
   190→  if (diff < 60_000) return 'just now';
   191→  if (diff < 3_600_000) return `${Math.floor(diff / 60_000)}m ago`;
   192→  if (diff < 86_400_000) return `${Math.floor(diff / 3_600_000)}h ago`;
   193→  if (diff < 604_800_000) return `${Math.floor(diff / 86_400_000)}d ago`;
   194→
   195→  return new Date(timestampMs).toLocaleDateString('en-US', {
   196→    month: 'short',
   197→    day: 'numeric',
   198→    year: 'numeric',
   199→  });
   200→}
   201→

     1→/**
     2→ * Format utilities for file browser display.
     3→ */
     4→
     5→/**
     6→ * Format bytes into human-readable string.
     7→ *
     8→ * @param bytes - Number of bytes
     9→ * @returns Formatted string like "1.2 MB", "456 KB", etc.
    10→ *
    11→ * @example
    12→ * formatBytes(1024) // "1 KB"
    13→ * formatBytes(1536) // "1.5 KB"
    14→ * formatBytes(1048576) // "1 MB"
    15→ */
    16→export function formatBytes(bytes: number): string {
    17→  if (bytes === 0) return '0 B';
    18→
    19→  const units = ['B', 'KB', 'MB', 'GB', 'TB'];
    20→  const k = 1024;
    21→  const i = Math.floor(Math.log(bytes) / Math.log(k));
    22→
    23→  // Use at most 1 decimal place, remove trailing zeros
    24→  const value = bytes / Math.pow(k, i);
    25→  const formatted = value % 1 === 0 ? value.toString() : value.toFixed(1);
    26→
    27→  return `${formatted} ${units[i]}`;
    28→}
    29→
    30→/**
    31→ * Format timestamp into locale-aware date string.
    32→ *
    33→ * @param timestamp - Unix timestamp in milliseconds
    34→ * @returns Formatted date string
    35→ *
    36→ * @example
    37→ * formatDate(1705852800000) // "Jan 21, 2024" (varies by locale)
    38→ */
    39→export function formatDate(timestamp: number): string {
    40→  const date = new Date(timestamp);
    41→
    42→  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(undefined, {
    43→    year: 'numeric',
    44→    month: 'short',
    45→    day: 'numeric',
    46→  }).format(date);
    47→}
    48→

     1→//! Recycle bin metadata types and encryption.
     2→//!
     3→//! Mirrors the TypeScript `RecycleBinMetadata` and `BinEntry` types from
     4→//! `@cipherbox/crypto` for cross-platform compatibility. Uses ECIES
     5→//! (same as DeviceRegistry) for encryption -- the user's secp256k1
     6→//! publicKey encrypts, privateKey decrypts.
     7→
     8→use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
     9→use thiserror::Error;
    10→
    11→use super::folder::{FilePointer, FolderEntry};
    12→
    13→#[derive(Debug, Error)]
    14→pub enum BinError {
    15→    #[error("Bin metadata encryption failed")]
    16→    EncryptionFailed,
    17→    #[error("Bin metadata decryption failed")]
    18→    DecryptionFailed,
    19→    #[error("Bin metadata serialization failed")]
    20→    SerializationFailed,
    21→    #[error("Bin metadata deserialization failed")]
    22→    DeserializationFailed,
    23→    #[error("Bin metadata validation failed: {0}")]
    24→    ValidationFailed(String),
    25→}
    26→
    27→/// Top-level recycle bin metadata structure.
    28→/// Encrypted as a whole blob via ECIES with the user's secp256k1 publicKey.
    29→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
    30→#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
    31→pub struct RecycleBinMetadata {
    32→    pub version: String,
    33→    pub sequence_number: u64,
    34→    pub entries: Vec<BinEntry>,
    35→}
    36→
    37→/// A single item in the recycle bin.
    38→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
    39→#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
    40→pub struct BinEntry {
    41→    pub id: String,
    42→    pub item_type: BinItemType,
    43→    pub name: String,
    44→    pub original_parent_ipns_name: String,
    45→    pub original_path: String,
    46→    pub deleted_at: u64,
    47→    pub size: u64,
    48→    pub mime_type: String,
    49→    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    50→    #[serde(default)]
    51→    pub file_pointer: Option<FilePointer>,
    52→    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    53→    #[serde(default)]
    54→    pub folder_entry: Option<FolderEntry>,
    55→}
    56→
    57→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
    58→#[serde(rename_all = "lowercase")]
    59→pub enum BinItemType {
    60→    File,
    61→    Folder,
    62→}
    63→
    64→/// Encrypt bin metadata with ECIES using user's secp256k1 public key.
    65→///
    66→/// JSON-serializes the metadata, then encrypts the entire blob with ECIES.
    67→/// Cross-compatible with TypeScript `encryptBinMetadata` from @cipherbox/crypto.
    68→pub fn encrypt_bin_metadata(
    69→    metadata: &RecycleBinMetadata,
    70→    user_public_key: &[u8],
    71→) -> Result<Vec<u8>, BinError> {
    72→    let json = serde_json::to_vec(metadata)
    73→        .map_err(|_| BinError::SerializationFailed)?;
    74→    crate::crypto::ecies::wrap_key(&json, user_public_key)
    75→        .map_err(|_| BinError::EncryptionFailed)
    76→}
    77→
    78→/// Decrypt bin metadata with ECIES using user's secp256k1 private key.
    79→pub fn decrypt_bin_metadata(
    80→    ciphertext: &[u8],
    81→    user_private_key: &[u8],
    82→) -> Result<RecycleBinMetadata, BinError> {
    83→    let plaintext = crate::crypto::ecies::unwrap_key(ciphertext, user_private_key)
    84→        .map_err(|_| BinError::DecryptionFailed)?;
    85→    let metadata: RecycleBinMetadata = serde_json::from_slice(&plaintext)
    86→        .map_err(|_| BinError::DeserializationFailed)?;
    87→    if metadata.version != "v1" {
    88→        return Err(BinError::ValidationFailed(
    89→            format!("Unsupported bin metadata version: {}", metadata.version),
    90→        ));
    91→    }
    92→    Ok(metadata)
    93→}
    94→
    95→/// Create a new empty RecycleBinMetadata.
    96→pub fn empty_bin_metadata() -> RecycleBinMetadata {
    97→    RecycleBinMetadata {
    98→        version: "v1".to_string(),
    99→        sequence_number: 0,
   100→        entries: vec![],
   101→    }
   102→}
   103→
   104→/// Generate a random UUID v4 string.
   105→/// Uses the same pattern as `registry/mod.rs::generate_uuid_v4`.
   106→pub fn generate_uuid_v4() -> String {
   107→    let bytes = crate::crypto::utils::generate_random_bytes(16);
   108→    format!(
   109→        "{:02x}{:02x}{:02x}{:02x}-{:02x}{:02x}-4{:01x}{:02x}-{:02x}{:02x}-{:02x}{:02x}{:02x}{:02x}{:02x}{:02x}",
   110→        bytes[0], bytes[1], bytes[2], bytes[3],
   111→        bytes[4], bytes[5],
   112→        bytes[6] & 0x0f, bytes[7],
   113→        (bytes[8] & 0x3f) | 0x80, bytes[9],
   114→        bytes[10], bytes[11], bytes[12], bytes[13], bytes[14], bytes[15],
   115→    )
   116→}
   117→
   118→/// Guess MIME type from file extension.
   119→/// Simple inline mapping to avoid adding `mime_guess` dependency.
   120→pub fn guess_mime_type(filename: &str) -> &'static str {
   121→    let ext = filename
   122→        .rsplit('.')
   123→        .next()
   124→        .unwrap_or("")
   125→        .to_ascii_lowercase();
   126→    match ext.as_str() {
   127→        "txt" => "text/plain",
   128→        "html" | "htm" => "text/html",
   129→        "css" => "text/css",
   130→        "js" | "mjs" => "application/javascript",
   131→        "json" => "application/json",
   132→        "xml" => "application/xml",
   133→        "pdf" => "application/pdf",
   134→        "zip" => "application/zip",
   135→        "gz" | "gzip" => "application/gzip",
   136→        "tar" => "application/x-tar",
   137→        "7z" => "application/x-7z-compressed",
   138→        "rar" => "application/x-rar-compressed",
   139→        "doc" => "application/msword",
   140→        "docx" => "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document",
   141→        "xls" => "application/vnd.ms-excel",
   142→        "xlsx" => "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet",
   143→        "ppt" => "application/vnd.ms-powerpoint",
   144→        "pptx" => "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.presentationml.presentation",
   145→        "png" => "image/png",
   146→        "jpg" | "jpeg" => "image/jpeg",
   147→        "gif" => "image/gif",
   148→        "svg" => "image/svg+xml",
   149→        "webp" => "image/webp",
   150→        "ico" => "image/x-icon",

Now I have all the context. Let me start fixing issues.

**Task 1: Fix sequence number double-increment bug**

The bug: `addToBin` computes `newSeq = store.seq + 1`, publishes with it, then calls `store.addEntry()` which increments *again*. Same pattern in `removeEntriesFromBin`. Fix: use `store.setEntries()` (which takes explicit seq) instead of auto-incrementing store actions.

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01J9DdhbBw9sH1yYUax2opiA
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/services/bin.service.ts",
  "old_string": "  // Encrypt and publish\n  await saveBinMetadata({ metadata, userPublicKey, userPrivateKey });\n\n  // Update local store\n  store.addEntry(entry);",
  "new_string": "  // Encrypt and publish\n  await saveBinMetadata({ metadata, userPublicKey, userPrivateKey });\n\n  // Update local store (use setEntries with explicit seq to avoid double-increment)\n  store.setEntries(currentEntries, newSeq);"
}
```

     1→/**
     2→ * Format utilities for file browser display.
     3→ */
     4→
     5→/**
     6→ * Format bytes into human-readable string.
     7→ *
     8→ * @param bytes - Number of bytes
     9→ * @returns Formatted string like "1.2 MB", "456 KB", etc.
    10→ *
    11→ * @example
    12→ * formatBytes(1024) // "1 KB"
    13→ * formatBytes(1536) // "1.5 KB"
    14→ * formatBytes(1048576) // "1 MB"
    15→ */
    16→export function formatBytes(bytes: number): string {
    17→  if (bytes === 0) return '0 B';
    18→
    19→  const units = ['B', 'KB', 'MB', 'GB', 'TB'];
    20→  const k = 1024;
    21→  const i = Math.floor(Math.log(bytes) / Math.log(k));
    22→
    23→  // Use at most 1 decimal place, remove trailing zeros
    24→  const value = bytes / Math.pow(k, i);
    25→  const formatted = value % 1 === 0 ? value.toString() : value.toFixed(1);
    26→
    27→  return `${formatted} ${units[i]}`;
    28→}
    29→
    30→/**
    31→ * Format timestamp into locale-aware date string.
    32→ *
    33→ * @param timestamp - Unix timestamp in milliseconds
    34→ * @returns Formatted date string
    35→ *
    36→ * @example
    37→ * formatDate(1705852800000) // "Jan 21, 2024" (varies by locale)
    38→ */
    39→export function formatDate(timestamp: number): string {
    40→  const date = new Date(timestamp);
    41→
    42→  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(undefined, {
    43→    year: 'numeric',
    44→    month: 'short',
    45→    day: 'numeric',
    46→  }).format(date);
    47→}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/utils/format.ts",
  "old_string": "export function formatDate(timestamp: number): string {\n  const date = new Date(timestamp);\n\n  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(undefined, {\n    year: 'numeric',\n    month: 'short',\n    day: 'numeric',\n  }).format(date);\n}",
  "new_string": "export function formatDate(timestamp: number): string {\n  const date = new Date(timestamp);\n\n  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(undefined, {\n    year: 'numeric',\n    month: 'short',\n    day: 'numeric',\n  }).format(date);\n}\n\n/**\n * Format a Unix ms timestamp as a relative time string.\n *\n * Returns \"just now\", \"Xm ago\", \"Xh ago\", \"Xd ago\" for recent timestamps.\n * Falls back to a locale-formatted date for timestamps older than 7 days.\n */\nexport function formatRelativeTime(timestampMs: number): string {\n  const diff = Date.now() - timestampMs;\n\n  if (diff < 60_000) return 'just now';\n  if (diff < 3_600_000) return `${Math.floor(diff / 60_000)}m ago`;\n  if (diff < 86_400_000) return `${Math.floor(diff / 3_600_000)}h ago`;\n  if (diff < 604_800_000) {\n    const days = Math.floor(diff / 86_400_000);\n    return days === 1 ? '1 day ago' : `${days} days ago`;\n  }\n\n  return new Date(timestampMs).toLocaleDateString('en-US', {\n    month: 'short',\n    day: 'numeric',\n    year: 'numeric',\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * Get a terminal-style type indicator based on item type and optional MIME type.\n */\nexport function getItemIcon(itemType: 'file' | 'folder', mimeType?: string): string {\n  if (itemType === 'folder') return '[DIR]';\n  if (!mimeType) return '[FILE]';\n  if (mimeType.startsWith('image/')) return '[IMG]';\n  if (mimeType.startsWith('video/')) return '[VID]';\n  if (mimeType.startsWith('audio/')) return '[AUD]';\n  if (mimeType.startsWith('text/')) return '[TXT]';\n  if (mimeType === 'application/pdf') return '[PDF]';\n  return '[FILE]';\n}"
}

     1→//! Utility functions for cryptographic operations.

     3→use rand::RngCore;
     4→use thiserror::Error;
     5→use zeroize::Zeroize;
     6→
     7→use super::aes::{AES_IV_SIZE, AES_KEY_SIZE};
     8→
     9→#[derive(Debug, Error)]
    10→pub enum UtilError {
    11→    #[error("Invalid hex string")]
    12→    InvalidHex,
    13→}
    14→
    15→/// Generate cryptographically secure random bytes.
    16→pub fn generate_random_bytes(len: usize) -> Vec<u8> {
    17→    let mut buf = vec![0u8; len];
    18→    rand::rngs::OsRng.fill_bytes(&mut buf);
    19→    buf
    20→}
    21→
    22→/// Generate a random 32-byte AES key.
    23→pub fn generate_file_key() -> [u8; AES_KEY_SIZE] {
    24→    let mut key = [0u8; AES_KEY_SIZE];
    25→    rand::rngs::OsRng.fill_bytes(&mut key);
    26→    key
    27→}
    28→
    29→/// Generate a random 12-byte IV.
    30→pub fn generate_iv() -> [u8; AES_IV_SIZE] {
    31→    let mut iv = [0u8; AES_IV_SIZE];
    32→    rand::rngs::OsRng.fill_bytes(&mut iv);
    33→    iv
    34→}
    35→
    36→/// Convert a hex string to bytes.

{\n    let bytes = generate_random_bytes(16);\n    format!(\n        \"{:02x}{:02x}{:02x}{:02x}-{:02x}{:02x}-4{:01x}{:02x}-{:02x}{:02x}-{:02x}{:02x}{:02x}{:02x}{:02x}{:02x}\",\n        bytes[0],   "old_string": "/// Detect MIME type from file extension.\npub fn mime_from_extension(filename: &str) -> String {\n    let ext = filename.rsplit('.').next().unwrap_or(\"\").to_lowercase();\n    match ext.as_str() {\n        \"jpg\" | \"jpeg\" => \"image/jpeg\",\n        \"png\" => \"image/png\",\n        \"gif\" => \"image/gif\",\n        \"webp\" => \"image/webp\",\n        \"svg\" => \"image/svg+xml\",\n        \"bmp\" => \"image/bmp\",\n        \"ico\" => \"image/x-icon\",\n        \"pdf\" => \"application/pdf\",\n        \"mp4\" => \"video/mp4\",\n        \"webm\" => \"video/webm\",\n        \"mov\" => \"video/quicktime\",\n        \"avi\" => \"video/x-msvideo\",\n        \"mkv\" => \"video/x-matroska\",\n        \"mp3\" => \"audio/mpeg\",\n        \"wav\" => \"audio/wav\",\n        \"ogg\" => \"audio/ogg\",\n        \"flac\" => \"audio/flac\",\n        \"aac\" => \"audio/aac\",\n        \"txt\" => \"text/plain\",\n        \"html\" | \"htm\" => \"text/html\",\n        \"css\" => \"text/css\",\n        \"js\" => \"application/javascript\",\n        \"json\" => \"application/json\",\n        \"xml\" => \"application/xml\",\n        \"zip\" => \"application/zip\",\n        \"gz\" | \"gzip\" => \"application/gzip\",\n        \"tar\" => \"application/x-tar\",\n        \"doc\" => \"application/msword\",\n        \"docx\" => \"application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document\",\n        \"xls\" => \"application/vnd.ms-excel\",\n        \"xlsx\" => \"application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet\",\n        \"ppt\" => \"application/vnd.ms-powerpoint\",\n        \"pptx\" => \"application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.presentationml.presentation\",\n        \"md\" => \"text/markdown\",\n        _ => \"application/octet-stream\",\n    }\n    .to_string()\n}",
  "new_string": "/// Detect MIME type from file extension.\n///\n/// Delegates to the shared implementation in `crate::crypto::utils`.\npub fn mime_from_extension(filename: &str) -> String {\n    crate::crypto::utils::mime_from_extension(filename).to_string()\n}"
}

  1443→fn bin_generate_uuid_v4_format() {
  1444→    let uuid = bin::generate_uuid_v4();
  1445→    // UUID v4 format: 8-4-4-4-12 hex chars
  1446→    assert_eq!(uuid.len(), 36, "UUID should be 36 chars");
  1447→    assert_eq!(&uuid[8..9], "-");
  1448→    assert_eq!(&uuid[13..14], "-");
  1449→    assert_eq!(&uuid[14..15], "4", "Version nibble should be 4");
  1450→    assert_eq!(&uuid[18..19], "-");
  1451→    assert_eq!(&uuid[23..24], "-");
  1452→
  1453→    // Variant bits (position 19) should be 8, 9, a, or b
  1454→    let variant_char = uuid.chars().nth(19).unwrap();
  1455→    assert!(
  1456→        "89ab".contains(variant_char),
  1457→        "Variant char should be 8/9/a/b, got {}",
  1458→        variant_char
  1459→    );
  1460→}

   436→/**
   437→ * Cleanup CIDs for a bin entry (unpin from IPFS + update quota).
   438→ *
   439→ * Files: resolve file IPNS -> get CID -> unpin
   440→ * Folders: recursively resolve descendant file CIDs -> unpin all
   441→ */
   442→async function cleanupEntryCids(entry: BinEntry): Promise<void> {
   443→  if (entry.itemType === 'file' && entry.filePointer) {
   444→    const fileIpnsName = entry.filePointer.fileMetaIpnsName;
   445→    if (fileIpnsName) {
   446→      try {
   447→        const resolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(fileIpnsName);
   448→        if (resolved?.cid) {
   449→          // Fetch file metadata to get the actual content CID and size
   450→          const metaBytes = await fetchFromIpfs(resolved.cid);
   451→          const metaJson = new TextDecoder().decode(metaBytes);
   452→          const fileMeta = JSON.parse(metaJson);
   453→
   454→          // Unpin the content CID (encrypted file data)
   455→          if (fileMeta.cid) {
   456→            await unpinFromIpfs(fileMeta.cid);
   457→            // Update quota
   458→            if (fileMeta.size && typeof fileMeta.size === 'number') {
   459→              useQuotaStore.getState().removeUsage(fileMeta.size);
   460→            }
   461→          }
   462→
   463→          // Also unpin the metadata CID
   464→          await unpinFromIpfs(resolved.cid).catch(() => {});
   465→        }
   466→      } catch (err) {
   467→        console.error(`[Bin] CID cleanup failed for file ${entry.name}:`, err);
   468→      }
   469→    }
   470→  } else if (entry.itemType === 'folder' && entry.folderEntry) {
   471→    // Recursively resolve folder to find all descendant file CIDs
   472→    await cleanupFolderCids(entry.folderEntry.ipnsName);
   473→  }
   474→}
   475→
   476→/**
   477→ * Recursively unpin all CIDs in a folder tree.
   478→ */
   479→async function cleanupFolderCids(folderIpnsName: string): Promise<void> {
   480→  try {
   481→    const resolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(folderIpnsName);
   482→    if (!resolved?.cid) return;
   483→
   484→    // Try to load folder metadata -- it may be AES-GCM encrypted
   485→    // We don't have the folder key here, so we check if the folder is
   486→    // loaded in the store first
   487→    const folders = useFolderStore.getState().folders;
   488→    const folderNode = Object.values(folders).find((f) => f.ipnsName === folderIpnsName);
   489→
   490→    if (folderNode) {
   491→      // Folder is loaded -- iterate children
   492→      for (const child of folderNode.children) {
   493→        if (child.type === 'file') {
   494→          const fp = child as FilePointer;
   495→          if (fp.fileMetaIpnsName) {
   496→            try {
   497→              const fileResolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(fp.fileMetaIpnsName);
   498→              if (fileResolved?.cid) {
   499→                const metaBytes = await fetchFromIpfs(fileResolved.cid);
   500→                const metaJson = new TextDecoder().decode(metaBytes);
   501→                const fileMeta = JSON.parse(metaJson);
   502→                if (fileMeta.cid) {
   503→                  await unpinFromIpfs(fileMeta.cid);
   504→                  if (fileMeta.size && typeof fileMeta.size === 'number') {
   505→                    useQuotaStore.getState().removeUsage(fileMeta.size);
   506→                  }
   507→                }
   508→                await unpinFromIpfs(fileResolved.cid).catch(() => {});
   509→              }
   510→            } catch (err) {
   511→              console.error(`[Bin] CID cleanup failed for nested file:`, err);
   512→            }
   513→          }
   514→        } else if (child.type === 'folder') {
   515→          const fe = child as FolderEntry;
   516→          await cleanupFolderCids(fe.ipnsName);
   517→        }
   518→      }
   519→    }
   520→
   521→    // Unpin the folder metadata CID itself
   522→    await unpinFromIpfs(resolved.cid).catch(() => {});
   523→  } catch (err) {
   524→    console.error(`[Bin] Folder CID cleanup failed for ${folderIpnsName}:`, err);
   525→  }
```

     2→ * Bin Service - Recycle bin metadata IPNS lifecycle
     3→ *
     4→ * Manages the encrypted bin metadata: initialize on login, add entries on delete,
     5→ * restore items, permanent delete (with CID unpin + quota update), empty bin,
     6→ * and auto-purge expired entries.

> AGENT

Clean. Here's a summary of everything fixed:

## Summary

### Bug Fix (Critical)
- **Sequence number double-increment in `addToBin` and `removeEntriesFromBin`**: Service computed `newSeq = seq + 1` for published metadata, then store actions (`addEntry`/`removeEntries`) incremented *again*. After a single add, published metadata = N+1 but store = N+2, causing permanent drift. Fixed by using `store.setEntries()` with explicit sequence number instead.

### Dead Code Removed
- **`addEntry`, `removeEntry`, `removeEntries`, `setLoaded`** actions from bin store — all unused after the bug fix (or never called at all in the case of `removeEntry` and `setLoaded`).

### Code Deduplication
- **`formatRelativeTime`**: Extracted from BinListItem + AuthorizedDevices into shared `utils/format.ts`
- **`getItemIcon`**: Extracted from BinListItem + FileListItem into shared `utils/format.ts` (now takes `itemType` + optional `mimeType` params)
- **`generate_uuid_v4` (Rust)**: Moved from duplicated copies in `registry/mod.rs` and `crypto/bin.rs` to shared `crypto/utils.rs`
- **MIME type lookup (Rust)**: Merged `guess_mime_type` (bin.rs) and `mime_from_extension` (fuse/helpers.rs) into a single implementation in `crypto/utils.rs` using `eq_ignore_ascii_case` (no heap allocation)
- **File CID cleanup logic**: Extracted duplicated IPNS resolve + fetch + unpin + quota-update block into `unpinFileCids()` helper, called by both `cleanupEntryCids` and `cleanupFolderCids`

### Version String Constant
- Replaced 3 hardcoded `version: 'v1'` literals with `BIN_METADATA_VERSION` constant

### Efficiency Fixes
- **Redundant `deriveBinIpnsKeypair` in `initializeBin`**: Removed the upfront derivation that was immediately repeated inside `loadBinMetadata`. Now only derives once (inside `loadBinMetadata`), or once more only if no bin exists yet.
- **Redundant `SystemTime::now()` in `handle_unlink` and `handle_rmdir`**: Captured a single `now` timestamp and reused for parent mtime/ctime and bin entry `deleted_at`.
- **Heap allocation in MIME lookup**: Replaced `to_ascii_lowercase()` with `eq_ignore_ascii_case` comparisons in the shared MIME function (avoids String allocation on FUSE thread).

### Skipped (not worth fixing in this pass)
- Selection logic duplication (BinBrowser vs FileBrowser) — would need a `useMultiSelection` hook extraction touching existing FileBrowser
- Hand-rolled context menu — would need generifying the existing `ContextMenu` component's type parameter
- Sequential N+1 in batch operations — architectural issue requiring batch API design
- Test helper `generateTestKeypair` duplication — pre-existing across 5 test files, not introduced by this PR

    37→pub async fn resolve_ipns(
    38→    client: &ApiClient,
    39→    ipns_name: &str,
    40→) -> Result<IpnsResolveResponse, String> {
    41→    let path = format!("/ipns/resolve?ipnsName={}", urlencoding::encode(ipns_name));

    43→        .authenticated_get(&path)
    44→        .await
    45→        .map_err(|e| format!("IPNS resolve failed: {}", e))?;
    46→
    47→    if resp.status().as_u16() == 404 {
    48→        return Err("IPNS name not found".to_string());
    49→    }
    50→
    51→    if !resp.status().is_success() {
    52→        let status = resp.status();
    53→        let body = resp.text().await.unwrap_or_default();
    54→        return Err(format!("IPNS resolve failed ({}): {}", status, body));
    55→    }
    56→
    57→    let resolve_resp: IpnsResolveResponse = resp
    58→        .json()
    59→        .await
    60→        .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to parse IPNS resolve response: {}", e))?;
    61→
    62→    Ok(resolve_resp)
    63→}

    66→#[derive(Debug, serde::Serialize)]

   172→/**
   173→ * Add a deleted item to the recycle bin.

   179→ * @param params.parentIpnsName - IPNS name of the original parent folder
   180→ * @param params.parentPath - Breadcrumb path at deletion time (e.g., "My Vault / Documents")
   181→ * @param params.userPublicKey - User's secp256k1 public key for ECIES encryption
   182→ * @param params.userPrivateKey - User's secp256k1 private key for IPNS derivation
   183→ */
   184→export async function addToBin(params: {
   185→  item: FolderChild;
   186→  parentIpnsName: string;
   187→  parentPath: string;
   188→  userPublicKey: Uint8Array;

   199→    originalParentIpnsName: parentIpnsName,
   200→    originalPath: parentPath,

   203→    mimeType: '',
   204→    filePointer: isFile ? (item as FilePointer) : undefined,
   205→    folderEntry: !isFile ? (item as FolderEntry) : undefined,
   206→  };
   207→
   208→  // Read current bin state and build updated metadata
   209→  const store = useBinStore.getState();
   210→  const currentEntries = [...store.entries, entry];
   211→  const newSeq = store.sequenceNumber + 1;
   212→
   213→  const metadata: RecycleBinMetadata = {
   214→    version: BIN_METADATA_VERSION,
   215→    sequenceNumber: newSeq,
   216→    entries: currentEntries,
   217→  };

   219→  // Encrypt and publish

  "new_string": "// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Public API: addManyToBin\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/**\n * Add multiple deleted items to the recycle bin in a single IPNS publish.\n *\n * Unlike calling addToBin in a loop (which publishes per-item), this batches\n * all entries into one metadata update.\n */\nexport async function addManyToBin(params: {\n  items: FolderChild[];\n  parentIpnsName: string;\n  parentPath: string;\n  userPublicKey: Uint8Array;\n  userPrivateKey: Uint8Array;\n}): Promise<void> {\n  const { items, parentIpnsName, parentPath, userPublicKey, userPrivateKey } = params;\n  if (items.length === 0) return;\n\n  const now = Date.now();\n  const newEntries: BinEntry[] = items.map((item) => {\n    const isFile = item.type === 'file';\n    return {\n      id: crypto.randomUUID(),\n      itemType: isFile ? 'file' : 'folder',\n      name: item.name,\n      originalParentIpnsName: parentIpnsName,\n      originalPath: parentPath,\n      deletedAt: now,\n      size: 0,\n      mimeType: '',\n      filePointer: isFile ? (item as FilePointer) : undefined,\n      folderEntry: !isFile ? (item as FolderEntry) : undefined,\n    };\n  });\n\n  const store = useBinStore.getState();\n  const currentEntries = [...store.entries, ...newEntries];\n  const newSeq = store.sequenceNumber + 1;\n\n  const metadata: RecycleBinMetadata = {\n    version: BIN_METADATA_VERSION,\n    sequenceNumber: newSeq,\n    entries: currentEntries,\n  };\n\n  await saveBinMetadata({ metadata, userPublicKey, userPrivateKey });\n  store.setEntries(currentEntries, newSeq);\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Public API: restoreFromBin\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------"
}

   358→/**
   359→ * Permanently delete a bin entry: unpin CIDs and update quota.
   360→ *
   361→ * For files: resolves file IPNS -> gets CID -> unpins.
   362→ * For folders: recursively resolves all descendant file CIDs -> unpins all.
   363→ */
   364→export async function permanentlyDelete(params: {
   365→  entryId: string;
   366→  userPublicKey: Uint8Array;
   367→  userPrivateKey: Uint8Array;

   373→  if (!entry) throw new Error('Bin entry not found');
   374→
   375→  // Perform CID cleanup
   376→  await cleanupEntryCids(entry);
   377→

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/services/bin.service.ts",
  "old_string": "// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Public API: emptyBin\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------",
  "new_string": "// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Public API: permanentlyDeleteBatch\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/**\n * Permanently delete multiple bin entries in a single IPNS publish.\n *\n * Cleans up CIDs for all entries (best-effort), then publishes once.\n */\nexport async function permanentlyDeleteBatch(params: {\n  entryIds: string[];\n  userPublicKey: Uint8Array;\n  userPrivateKey: Uint8Array;\n}): Promise<void> {\n  const { entryIds, userPublicKey, userPrivateKey } = params;\n  if (entryIds.length === 0) return;\n\n  const binStore = useBinStore.getState();\n  const idsSet = new Set(entryIds);\n  const entriesToDelete = binStore.entries.filter((e) => idsSet.has(e.id));\n\n  // Cleanup CIDs for all entries (best-effort, continue on individual failures)\n  for (const entry of entriesToDelete) {\n    try {\n      await cleanupEntryCids(entry);\n    } catch (err) {\n      console.error(`[Bin] CID cleanup failed for ${entry.name}:`, err);\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Remove all from bin metadata in a single publish\n  await removeEntriesFromBin(entryIds, { userPublicKey, userPrivateKey });\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Public API: emptyBin\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------"
}

  "new_string": "  // Remove entry from bin metadata and publish\n  await removeEntriesFromBin([entryId], { userPublicKey, userPrivateKey });\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Public API: restoreFromBinBatch\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/**\n * Restore multiple bin entries, publishing to the bin IPNS only once at the end.\n *\n * Each item is restored to its target folder (which may each require a folder\n * publish), but the bin metadata is updated in a single publish.\n */\nexport async function restoreFromBinBatch(params: {\n  entryIds: string[];\n  userPublicKey: Uint8Array;\n  userPrivateKey: Uint8Array;\n}): Promise<void> {\n  const { entryIds, userPublicKey, userPrivateKey } = params;\n  if (entryIds.length === 0) return;\n\n  const binStore = useBinStore.getState();\n  const { updateFolderMetadata } = await import('./folder.service');\n\n  const restoredIds: string[] = [];\n\n  for (const entryId of entryIds) {\n    const entry = binStore.entries.find((e) => e.id === entryId);\n    if (!entry) continue;\n\n    try {\n      const targetFolder = await resolveTargetFolder(entry.originalParentIpnsName, 0, {\n        userPublicKey,\n        userPrivateKey,\n      });\n\n      const child = getRestoredChild(entry);\n      const finalChild = resolveNameCollision(child, targetFolder.children);\n\n      const updatedChildren = [...targetFolder.children, finalChild];\n      const { newSequenceNumber } = await updateFolderMetadata({\n        folderId: targetFolder.id,\n        children: updatedChildren,\n        folderKey: targetFolder.folderKey,\n        ipnsPrivateKey: targetFolder.ipnsPrivateKey,\n        ipnsName: targetFolder.ipnsName,\n        sequenceNumber: targetFolder.sequenceNumber,\n      });\n\n      const folderStore = useFolderStore.getState();\n      folderStore.updateFolderChildren(targetFolder.id, updatedChildren);\n      folderStore.updateFolderSequence(targetFolder.id, newSequenceNumber);\n\n      if (entry.itemType === 'folder' && entry.folderEntry) {\n        const existingNode = folderStore.folders[entry.folderEntry.id];\n        if (!existingNode) {\n          folderStore.setFolder({\n            id: entry.folderEntry.id,\n            name: finalChild.name,\n            ipnsName: entry.folderEntry.ipnsName,\n            parentId: targetFolder.id,\n            children: [],\n            isLoaded: false,\n            isLoading: false,\n            sequenceNumber: 0n,\n            folderKey: new Uint8Array(32),\n            ipnsPrivateKey: new Uint8Array(64),\n          });\n        }\n      }\n\n      restoredIds.push(entryId);\n    } catch (err) {\n      console.error(`[Bin] Failed to restore ${entry.name}:`, err);\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Single bin IPNS publish for all restored entries\n  if (restoredIds.length > 0) {\n    await removeEntriesFromBin(restoredIds, { userPublicKey, userPrivateKey });\n  }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Public API: permanentlyDelete\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------"
}

   173→  /**

   633→            userPrivateKey: auth.vaultKeypair.privateKey,

   636→          });
   637→        }
   638→
   639→        setState({ isLoading: false, error: null });
   640→      } catch (err) {
   641→        const error = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to delete';
   642→        setState({ isLoading: false, error });
   643→        throw err;
   644→      }

    50→ * Format a Unix ms timestamp as a relative time string.
    51→ *
    52→ * Returns "just now", "Xm ago", "Xh ago", "Xd ago" for recent timestamps.
    53→ * Falls back to a locale-formatted date for timestamps older than 7 days.
    54→ */
    55→export function formatRelativeTime(timestampMs: number): string {
    56→  const diff = Date.now() - timestampMs;
    57→
    58→  if (diff < 60_000) return 'just now';
    59→  if (diff < 3_600_000) return `${Math.floor(diff / 60_000)}m ago`;
    60→  if (diff < 86_400_000) return `${Math.floor(diff / 3_600_000)}h ago`;
    61→  if (diff < 604_800_000) {
    62→    const days = Math.floor(diff / 86_400_000);
    63→    return days === 1 ? '1 day ago' : `${days} days ago`;
    64→  }
    65→
    66→  return new Date(timestampMs).toLocaleDateString('en-US', {
    67→    month: 'short',
    68→    day: 'numeric',
    69→    year: 'numeric',
    70→  });
    71→}
    72→
    73→/**
    74→ * Get a terminal-style type indicator based on item type and optional MIME type.
    75→ */
    76→export function getItemIcon(itemType: 'file' | 'folder', mimeType?: string): string {
    77→  if (itemType === 'folder') return '[DIR]';
    78→  if (!mimeType) return '[FILE]';
    79→  if (mimeType.startsWith('image/')) return '[IMG]';

   685→ * Resolve the target folder for a restore operation.
   686→ *
   687→ * Walks the folder store to find a folder with the given IPNS name.
   688→ * If not found, checks if the parent is in the bin (recursive restore).
   689→ * Falls back to root folder.
   690→ */
   691→async function resolveTargetFolder(
   692→  parentIpnsName: string,
   693→  depth: number,
   694→  params: { userPublicKey: Uint8Array; userPrivateKey: Uint8Array }
   695→): Promise<FolderNode> {
   696→  // Max recursion depth to prevent infinite loops
   697→  if (depth > 5) {
   698→    console.warn('[Bin] Max restore depth reached, falling back to root');
   699→    return getRootFolder();
   700→  }
   701→
   702→  // Look up folder by IPNS name in folder store
   703→  const folders = useFolderStore.getState().folders;
   704→  const targetFolder = Object.values(folders).find((f) => f.ipnsName === parentIpnsName);
   705→
   706→  if (targetFolder) {
   707→    return targetFolder;
   708→  }
   709→
   710→  // Check if parent is in the bin
   711→  const binStore = useBinStore.getState();
   712→  const parentBinEntry = binStore.entries.find(
   713→    (e) => e.itemType === 'folder' && e.folderEntry && e.folderEntry.ipnsName === parentIpnsName
   714→  );
   715→
   716→  if (parentBinEntry) {
   717→    // Restore parent first (recursive)
   718→    console.log(`[Bin] Restoring parent folder "${parentBinEntry.name}" first`);
   719→    await restoreFromBin({
   720→      entryId: parentBinEntry.id,
   721→      userPublicKey: params.userPublicKey,
   722→      userPrivateKey: params.userPrivateKey,
   723→    });
   724→
   725→    // After restoring parent, try to find it again
   726→    const updatedFolders = useFolderStore.getState().folders;
   727→    const restoredParent = Object.values(updatedFolders).find((f) => f.ipnsName === parentIpnsName);
   728→    if (restoredParent) {
   729→      return restoredParent;
   730→    }
   731→  }
   732→
   733→  // Parent truly gone -- restore to root
   734→  console.warn(`[Bin] Original parent not found (${parentIpnsName}), restoring to root`);

   226→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   227→// Public API: addManyToBin
   228→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   229→
   230→/**
   231→ * Add multiple deleted items to the recycle bin in a single IPNS publish.
   232→ *
   233→ * Unlike calling addToBin in a loop (which publishes per-item), this batches
   234→ * all entries into one metadata update.
   235→ */
   236→export async function addManyToBin(params: {
   237→  items: FolderChild[];
   238→  parentIpnsName: string;
   239→  parentPath: string;
   240→  userPublicKey: Uint8Array;
   241→  userPrivateKey: Uint8Array;
   242→}): Promise<void> {
   243→  const { items, parentIpnsName, parentPath, userPublicKey, userPrivateKey } = params;
   244→  if (items.length === 0) return;
   245→
   246→  const now = Date.now();
   247→  const newEntries: BinEntry[] = items.map((item) => {
   248→    const isFile = item.type === 'file';
   249→    return {
   250→      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
   251→      itemType: isFile ? 'file' : 'folder',
   252→      name: item.name,
   253→      originalParentIpnsName: parentIpnsName,
   254→      originalPath: parentPath,
   255→      deletedAt: now,
   256→      size: 0,
   257→      mimeType: '',
   258→      filePointer: isFile ? (item as FilePointer) : undefined,
   259→      folderEntry: !isFile ? (item as FolderEntry) : undefined,
   260→    };
   261→  });
   262→
   263→  const store = useBinStore.getState();
   264→  const currentEntries = [...store.entries, ...newEntries];
   265→  const newSeq = store.sequenceNumber + 1;
   266→
   267→  const metadata: RecycleBinMetadata = {
   268→    version: BIN_METADATA_VERSION,
   269→    sequenceNumber: newSeq,
   270→    entries: currentEntries,
   271→  };
   272→
   273→  await saveBinMetadata({ metadata, userPublicKey, userPrivateKey });
   274→  store.setEntries(currentEntries, newSeq);
   275→}
   276→
   277→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   278→// Public API: restoreFromBin
   279→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   280→

   179→ * @param params.parentIpnsName - IPNS name of the original parent folder
   180→ * @param params.parentPath - Breadcrumb path at deletion time (e.g., "My Vault / Documents")

   182→ * @param params.userPrivateKey - User's secp256k1 private key for IPNS derivation

   184→export async function addToBin(params: {
   185→  item: FolderChild;
   186→  parentIpnsName: string;
   187→  parentPath: string;
   188→  userPublicKey: Uint8Array;
   189→  userPrivateKey: Uint8Array;
   190→}): Promise<void> {
   191→  const { item, parentIpnsName, parentPath, userPublicKey, userPrivateKey } = params;
   192→
   193→  // Build bin entry
   194→  const isFile = item.type === 'file';
   195→  const entry: BinEntry = {
   196→    id: crypto.randomUUID(),
   197→    itemType: isFile ? 'file' : 'folder',
   198→    name: item.name,
   199→    originalParentIpnsName: parentIpnsName,
   200→    originalPath: parentPath,
   201→    deletedAt: Date.now(),
   202→    size: 0, // Files: resolved lazily on permanent delete. Folders: 0.
   203→    mimeType: '',
   204→    filePointer: isFile ? (item as FilePointer) : undefined,
   205→    folderEntry: !isFile ? (item as FolderEntry) : undefined,
   206→  };

   208→  // Read current bin state and build updated metadata
   209→  const store = useBinStore.getState();

   213→  const metadata: RecycleBinMetadata = {
   214→    version: BIN_METADATA_VERSION,
   215→    sequenceNumber: newSeq,
   216→    entries: currentEntries,
   217→  };

   221→
   222→  // Update local store (use setEntries with explicit seq to avoid double-increment)
   223→  store.setEntries(currentEntries, newSeq);
   224→}

   233→ * Unlike calling addToBin in a loop (which publishes per-item), this batches

   237→  items: FolderChild[];

   241→  userPrivateKey: Uint8Array;

   285→// Public API: restoreFromBin
   286→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   287→
   288→/**
   289→ * Restore a bin entry to its original folder.
   290→ *
   291→ * Looks up the target folder by IPNS name. If found, re-adds the preserved
   292→ * FolderChild. Handles name collisions (appends " (restored)"). If original
   293→ * parent is also in the bin, attempts recursive parent restore (max depth 5).
   294→ * Falls back to root folder if parent is gone.
   295→ */
   296→export async function restoreFromBin(params: {
   297→  entryId: string;
   298→  userPublicKey: Uint8Array;
   299→  userPrivateKey: Uint8Array;
   300→}): Promise<void> {
   301→  const { entryId, userPublicKey, userPrivateKey } = params;
   302→
   303→  const binStore = useBinStore.getState();
   304→  const entry = binStore.entries.find((e) => e.id === entryId);
   305→  if (!entry) throw new Error('Bin entry not found');
   306→
   307→  // Resolve original parent folder
   308→  const targetFolder = await resolveTargetFolder(entry.originalParentIpnsName, 0, {
   309→    userPublicKey,
   310→    userPrivateKey,
   311→  });
   312→
   313→  // Get the FolderChild to restore
   314→  const child = getRestoredChild(entry);
   315→
   316→  // Check for name collisions in target folder and rename if needed
   317→  const finalChild = resolveNameCollision(child, targetFolder.children);
   318→
   319→  // Add child back to target folder
   320→  const { updateFolderMetadata } = await import('./folder.service');
   321→  const updatedChildren = [...targetFolder.children, finalChild];
   322→  const { newSequenceNumber } = await updateFolderMetadata({
   323→    folderId: targetFolder.id,
   324→    children: updatedChildren,
   325→    folderKey: targetFolder.folderKey,
   326→    ipnsPrivateKey: targetFolder.ipnsPrivateKey,
   327→    ipnsName: targetFolder.ipnsName,
   328→    sequenceNumber: targetFolder.sequenceNumber,
   329→  });
   330→
   331→  // Update folder store
   332→  const folderStore = useFolderStore.getState();
   333→  folderStore.updateFolderChildren(targetFolder.id, updatedChildren);
   334→  folderStore.updateFolderSequence(targetFolder.id, newSequenceNumber);
   335→
   336→  // If restoring a folder, re-add the folder node to the store
   337→  if (entry.itemType === 'folder' && entry.folderEntry) {
   338→    const existingNode = folderStore.folders[entry.folderEntry.id];

Errors are caught and logged, not thrown to callers.\n+ */\n+\n+import {\n+  deriveBinIpnsKeypair,\n+  encryptBinMetadata,\n+  decryptBinMetadata,\n+  bytesToHex,\n+  hexToBytes,\n+  wrapKey,\n+  type RecycleBinMetadata,\n+  type BinEntry,\n+  type FolderChild,\n+  type FilePointer,\n+  type FolderEntry,\n+} from '@cipherbox/crypto';\n+import { addToIpfs, fetchFromIpfs, unpinFromIpfs } from '../lib/api/ipfs';\n+import { createAndPublishIpnsRecord, resolveIpnsRecord } from './ipns.service';\n+import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth.store';\n+import { useBinStore } from '../stores/bin.store';\n+import { useFolderStore } from '../stores/folder.store';\n+import { useVaultStore } from '../stores/vault.store';\n+import { useQuotaStore } from '../stores/quota.store';\n+import type { FolderNode } from '../stores/folder.store';\n+\n+// Track whether TEE enrollment has been done for this session\n+let binTeeEnrolled = false;\n+\n+/** Current bin metadata version. */\n+const BIN_METADATA_VERSION = 'v1' as const;\n+\n+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n+// Internal helpers: load / save bin metadata\n+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n+\n+/**\n+ * Load and decrypt bin metadata from IPNS.\n+ *\n+ * @returns Decrypted metadata and IPNS resolution data, or null if no bin exists yet\n+ */\n+async function loadBinMetadata(params: { userPrivateKey: Uint8Array }): Promise<{\n+  metadata: RecycleBinMetadata;\n+  ipnsName: string;\n+  sequenceNumber: bigint;\n+} | null> {\n+  const binIpns = await deriveBinIpnsKeypair(params.userPrivateKey);\n+  const resolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(binIpns.ipnsName);\n+\n+  if (!resolved) {\n+    return null;\n+  }\n+\n+  const encryptedBytes = await fetchFromIpfs(resolved.cid);\n+  const metadata = await decryptBinMetadata(encryptedBytes, params.userPrivateKey);\n+\n+  return {\n+    metadata,\n+    ipnsName: binIpns.ipnsName,\n+    sequenceNumber: resolved.sequenceNumber,\n+  };\n+}\n+\n+/**\n+ * Encrypt and publish bin metadata to IPNS.\n+ *\n+ * Handles TEE enrollment on first write.\n+ */\n+async function saveBinMetadata(params: {\n+  metadata: RecycleBinMetadata;\n+  userPublicKey: Uint8Array;\n+  userPrivateKey: Uint8Array;\n+}): Promise<void> {\n+  const binIpns = await deriveBinIpnsKeypair(params.userPrivateKey);\n+\n+  // Encrypt metadata with user's public key (ECIES)\n+  const encryptedBytes = await encryptBinMetadata(params.metadata, params.userPublicKey);\n+\n+  // Pin to IPFS\n+  const { cid } = await addToIpfs(new Blob([encryptedBytes as BlobPart]));\n+\n+  // TEE enrollment on first write (fire-and-forget)\n+  let encryptedIpnsKey: string | undefined;\n+  let keyEpoch: number | undefined;\n+\n+  if (!binTeeEnrolled) {\n+    const teeKeys = useAuthStore.getState().teeKeys;\n+    if (teeKeys?.currentPublicKey) {\n+      try {\n+        const wrappedKey = await wrapKey(binIpns.privateKey, hexToBytes(teeKeys.currentPublicKey));\n+        encryptedIpnsKey = bytesToHex(wrappedKey);\n+        keyEpoch = teeKeys.currentEpoch;\n+        binTeeEnrolled = true;\n+      } catch (err) {\n+        console.error('[Bin] TEE enrollment failed (non-blocking):', err);\n+      }\n+    }\n+  }\n+\n+  // Publish IPNS record\n+  await createAndPublishIpnsRecord({\n+    ipnsPrivateKey: binIpns.privateKey,\n+    ipnsName: binIpns.ipnsName,\n+    metadataCid: cid,\n+    sequenceNumber: BigInt(params.metadata.sequenceNumber),\n+    encryptedIpnsPrivateKey: encryptedIpnsKey,\n+    keyEpoch,\n+  });\n+}\n+\n+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n+// Public API: initializeBin\n+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n+\n+/**\n+ * Initialize or load the recycle bin on login.\n+ *\n+ * Derives the deterministic IPNS keypair, resolves existing bin metadata\n+ * from IPNS (or creates empty state), and populates the bin store.\n+ *\n+ * Non-blocking: errors are logged, not thrown.\n+ */\n+export async function initializeBin(params: {\n+  userPrivateKey: Uint8Array;\n+  userPublicKey: Uint8Array;\n+}): Promise<void> {\n+  const store = useBinStore.getState();\n+  store.setLoading(true);\n+\n+  try {\n+    // Reset TEE enrollment tracking for new session\n+    binTeeEnrolled = false;\n+\n+    // Try to load existing bin metadata (derives IPNS keypair internally)\n+    const loaded = await loadBinMetadata({ userPrivateKey: params.userPrivateKey });\n+\n+    // Set IPNS name from loaded result, or derive if no bin exists yet\n+    if (loaded) {\n+      store.setBinIpnsName(loaded.ipnsName);\n+    } else {\n+      const binIpns = await deriveBinIpnsKeypair(params.userPrivateKey);\n+      store.setBinIpnsName(binIpns.ipnsName);\n+    }\n+\n+    if (loaded) {\n+      store.setEntries(loaded.metadata.entries, loaded.metadata.sequenceNumber);\n+    } else {\n+      // No bin exists yet -- initialize with empty state\n+      store.setEntries([], 0);\n+    }\n+  } catch (error) {\n+    console.error('[Bin] Failed to initialize bin:', error);\n+    store.setError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Failed to initialize bin');\n+    store.setLoading(false);\n+  }\n+}\n+\n+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n+// Public API: addToBin\n+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n+\n+/**\n+ * Add await wrapKey(binIpns.privateKey, hexToBytes(teeKeys.currentPublicKey));\n+        encryptedIpnsKey = bytesToHex(wrappedKey);\n+        keyEpoch = teeKeys.currentEpoch;\n+        binTeeEnrolled =   "command": "# Thread 7: bin.service.ts:676 - cleanupFolderCids skips unloaded folders\ngh api repos/FSM1/cipher-box/pulls/262/comments \\\n  --field in_reply_to=2881657122 \\\n  --raw-field body='This is a known limitation of the zero-knowledge architecture. Folder metadata is encrypted with the parent'\\''s folderKey, which isn'\\''t available in the `cleanupFolderCids` context (we only have the IPNS name). Loading it would require fetching the encrypted metadata and then decrypting it, which requires the folderKey from the parent'\\''s encrypted metadata — creating a recursive key resolution problem.\n\nThe current approach is best-effort: if the folder is loaded in the store (which it will be for folders the user has navigated to), children are cleaned up. For unloaded deep subfolders, the folder'\\''s own IPNS CID is still unpinned (line 678), and the children'\\''s IPFS pins will eventually be garbage-collected by the pinning service when no longer referenced. The quota accounting is approximate but acceptable for v1.'",
  "description": "Reply to thread 7"
}

is a known limitation of the zero-knowledge architecture. Folder metadata is encrypted with the parent's folderKey, which isn't available in the `cleanupFolderCids` context (we only have the IPNS name). Loading it would require fetching the encrypted metadata and then decrypting it, which requires the folderKey from the parent's encrypted metadata — creating a recursive key resolution problem.\n\nThe current approach is best-effort: if the folder is loaded in the store (which it will be for folders the user has navigated to), children are cleaned up. For unloaded deep subfolders, the folder's own IPNS CID is still unpinned (line 678), and the children's IPFS pins will eventually be garbage-collected by the pinning service when no longer referenced. The quota accounting is approximate but acceptable for v1.","created_at":"2026-03-04T12:45:53Z","updated_at":"2026-03-04T12:45:53Z","html_url":"https://github.com/FSM1/cipher-box/pull/262#discussion_r2883604121","pull_request_url":"https://api.github.com/repos/FSM1/cipher-box/pulls/262","_links":{"self":{"href":"https://api.github.com/repos/FSM1/cipher-box/pulls/comments/2883604121"},"html":{"href":"https://github.com/FSM1/cipher-box/pull/262#discussion_r2883604121"},"pull_request":{"href":"https://api.github.com/repos/FSM1/cipher-box/pulls/262"}},"reactions":{"url":"https://api.github.com/repos/FSM1/cipher-box/pulls/comments/2883604121/reactions","total_count":0,"+1":0,"-1":0,"laugh":0,"hooray":0,"confused":0,"heart":0,"rocket":0,"eyes":0},"start_line":673,"original_start_line":659,"start_side":"RIGHT","line":690,"original_line":676,"side":"RIGHT","in_reply_to_id":2881657122,"author_association":"OWNER","original_position":676,"position":690,"subject_type":"line"}

   475→/// Add a BinEntry to the user's encrypted recycle bin IPNS record.
   476→///
   477→/// Background operation: reads existing bin metadata from IPNS (or creates empty),
   478→/// appends the new entry, encrypts with ECIES, uploads to IPFS, publishes IPNS.
   479→/// Fire-and-forget -- errors are logged but don't propagate.
   480→/// CIDs remain pinned on delete for recovery via the web app bin UI.
   481→#[cfg(any(feature = "fuse", feature = "winfsp"))]
   482→pub(crate) fn spawn_bin_entry_publish(
   483→    api: Arc<ApiClient>,
   484→    rt: tokio::runtime::Handle,
   485→    entry: crate::crypto::bin::BinEntry,
   486→    user_private_key: Vec<u8>,
   487→    user_public_key: Vec<u8>,
   488→    coordinator: Arc<PublishCoordinator>,
   489→) {
   490→    std::thread::spawn(move || {
   491→        let result = rt.block_on(async {
   492→            // 1. Derive the bin IPNS keypair from user's private key
   493→            let pk_arr: [u8; 32] = user_private_key.as_slice().try_into()
   494→                .map_err(|_| "Invalid private key length for bin derivation".to_string())?;
   495→            let (bin_ipns_private_key, _bin_ipns_public_key, bin_ipns_name) =
   496→                crate::crypto::hkdf::derive_bin_ipns_keypair(&pk_arr)
   497→                    .map_err(|e| format!("Bin IPNS derivation failed: {}", e))?;
   498→
   499→            // 2. Acquire per-IPNS publish lock
   500→            let lock = coordinator.get_lock(&bin_ipns_name);
   501→            let _guard = lock.lock().await;
   502→
   503→            // 3. Try to resolve existing bin metadata from IPNS
   504→            let (mut bin_metadata, existing_cid) = match crate::api::ipns::resolve_ipns(&api, &bin_ipns_name).await {
   505→                Ok(resp) => {
   506→                    // Fetch and decrypt existing bin metadata
   507→                    match crate::api::ipfs::fetch_content(&api, &resp.cid).await {
   508→                        Ok(bytes) => {
   509→                            match crate::crypto::bin::decrypt_bin_metadata(&bytes, &user_private_key) {
   510→                                Ok(meta) => (meta, Some(resp.cid)),
   511→                                Err(e) => {
   512→                                    // Cannot decrypt existing bin — do NOT overwrite with empty.
   513→                                    // The CID stays pinned so no data is lost.
   514→                                    log::warn!("Failed to decrypt existing bin metadata, skipping publish to preserve existing data: {}", e);
```

   480→/// CIDs remain pinned on delete for recovery via the web app bin UI.
   481→#[cfg(any(feature = "fuse", feature = "winfsp"))]
   482→pub(crate) fn spawn_bin_entry_publish(
   483→    api: Arc<ApiClient>,
   484→    rt: tokio::runtime::Handle,
   485→    entry: crate::crypto::bin::BinEntry,
   486→    user_private_key: Vec<u8>,
   487→    user_public_key: Vec<u8>,
   488→    coordinator: Arc<PublishCoordinator>,
   489→) {
   490→    std::thread::spawn(move || {
   491→        let result = rt.block_on(async {
   492→            // 1. Derive the bin IPNS keypair from user's private key
   493→            let pk_arr: [u8; 32] = user_private_key.as_slice().try_into()
   494→                .map_err(|_| "Invalid private key length for bin derivation".to_string())?;
   495→            let (bin_ipns_private_key, _bin_ipns_public_key, bin_ipns_name) =
   496→                crate::crypto::hkdf::derive_bin_ipns_keypair(&pk_arr)
   497→                    .map_err(|e| format!("Bin IPNS derivation failed: {}", e))?;
   498→
   499→            // 2. Acquire per-IPNS publish lock
   500→            let lock = coordinator.get_lock(&bin_ipns_name);
   501→            let _guard = lock.lock().await;
   502→
   503→            // 3. Try to resolve existing bin metadata from IPNS
   504→            let (mut bin_metadata, existing_cid) = match crate::api::ipns::resolve_ipns(&api, &bin_ipns_name).await {
   505→                Ok(resp) => {
   506→                    // Fetch and decrypt existing bin metadata
   507→                    match crate::api::ipfs::fetch_content(&api, &resp.cid).await {
   508→                        Ok(bytes) => {
   509→                            match crate::crypto::bin::decrypt_bin_metadata(&bytes, &user_private_key) {
   510→                                Ok(meta) => (meta, Some(resp.cid)),
   511→                                Err(e) => {
   512→                                    // Cannot decrypt existing bin — do NOT overwrite with empty.
   513→                                    // The CID stays pinned so no data is lost.
   514→                                    log::warn!("Failed to decrypt existing bin metadata, skipping publish to preserve existing data: {}", e);
   515→                                    return Err(format!("Bin decrypt failed, entry not published: {}", e));
   516→                                }
   517→                            }
   518→                        }
   519→                        Err(e) => {
   520→                            // Cannot fetch existing blob — do NOT overwrite with empty.
   521→                            log::warn!("Failed to fetch bin metadata blob, skipping publish to preserve existing data: {}", e);
   522→                            return Err(format!("Bin fetch failed, entry not published: {}", e));
   523→                        }
   524→                    }
   525→                }
   526→                Err(e) => {
   527→                    // Only treat explicit "not found" as first-time bin initialization.
   528→                    // Transient errors (network, 500, etc.) must NOT overwrite existing data.
   529→                    if e.to_lowercase().contains("not found") {
   530→                        (crate::crypto::bin::empty_bin_metadata(), None)
   531→                    } else {
   532→                        log::warn!("Failed to resolve bin IPNS, skipping publish to preserve existing data: {}", e);
   533→                        return Err(format!("Bin resolve failed, entry not published: {}", e));
   534→                    }
   535→                }
   536→            };
   537→
   538→            // 4. Add the new entry and increment sequence number
   539→            bin_metadata.sequence_number += 1;
   540→            bin_metadata.entries.push(entry);
   541→
   542→            // 5. Encrypt with ECIES using user's public key
   543→            let encrypted = crate::crypto::bin::encrypt_bin_metadata(&bin_metadata, &user_public_key)
   544→                .map_err(|e| format!("Bin metadata encryption failed: {}", e))?;
   545→
   546→            // 6. Upload to IPFS
   547→            let new_cid = crate::api::ipfs::upload_content(&api, &encrypted).await?;
   548→
   549→            // 7. Resolve current IPNS sequence and publish
   550→            let seq = coordinator.resolve_sequence(&api, &bin_ipns_name).await.unwrap_or(0);
   551→            let new_seq = seq + 1;
   552→
   553→            let bin_ipns_key_arr: [u8; 32] = bin_ipns_private_key.as_slice().try_into()
   554→                .map_err(|_| "Invalid bin IPNS key length".to_string())?;
   555→            let value = format!("/ipfs/{}", new_cid);
   556→            let record = crate::crypto::ipns::create_ipns_record(
   557→                &bin_ipns_key_arr, &value, new_seq, 86_400_000,
   558→            ).map_err(|e| format!("Bin IPNS record creation failed: {}", e))?;
   559→            let marshaled = crate::crypto::ipns::marshal_ipns_record(&record)
```

   946→    fn build_folder_path(fs: &CipherBoxFS, folder_ino: u64) -> String {
   947→        let mut parts = Vec::new();
   948→        let mut current = folder_ino;
   949→        for _ in 0..20 { // Safety limit to prevent infinite loops

   965→        }

    50→ * Format a Unix ms timestamp as a relative time string.
    51→ *
    52→ * Returns "just now", "Xm ago", "Xh ago", "Xd ago" for recent timestamps.
    53→ * Falls back to a locale-formatted date for timestamps older than 7 days.
    54→ */
    55→export function formatRelativeTime(timestampMs: number): string {
    56→  const diff = Date.now() - timestampMs;
    57→
    58→  if (diff < 60_000) return 'just now';
    59→  if (diff < 3_600_000) return `${Math.floor(diff / 60_000)}m ago`;
    60→  if (diff < 86_400_000) return `${Math.floor(diff / 3_600_000)}h ago`;
    61→  if (diff < 604_800_000) {
    62→    const days = Math.floor(diff / 86_400_000);
    63→    return days === 1 ? '1 day ago' : `${days} days ago`;
    64→  }
    65→
    66→  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(undefined, {
    67→    month: 'short',
    68→    day: 'numeric',
    69→    year: 'numeric',
    70→  }).format(new Date(timestampMs));
    71→}

     1→/**
     2→ * @cipherbox/crypto - Recycle Bin Metadata Types
     3→ *
     4→ * Type definitions for the encrypted recycle bin metadata stored on IPFS/IPNS.
     5→ * The bin tracks soft-deleted files and folders with time-limited retention,
     6→ * enabling recovery to the original vault location.
     7→ */
     8→
     9→import type { FilePointer } from '../file/types';
    10→import type { FolderEntry } from '../folder/types';
    11→
    12→/**
    13→ * Individual bin entry representing a soft-deleted item.
    14→ *
    15→ * Each entry preserves the full FolderChild (FilePointer or FolderEntry)
    16→ * that was removed from the parent folder's metadata, enabling restore
    17→ * by re-inserting it into the original parent.
    18→ */
    19→export type BinEntry = {
    20→  /** Unique ID for this bin entry (UUID) */
    21→  id: string;
    22→  /** Original item type */
    23→  itemType: 'file' | 'folder';
    24→  /** Display name at time of deletion */
    25→  name: string;
    26→  /** IPNS name of the original parent folder (for restore path resolution) */
    27→  originalParentIpnsName: string;
    28→  /** Full breadcrumb path at deletion time (e.g., "My Vault / Documents / Reports") */
    29→  originalPath: string;
    30→  /** When item was deleted (Unix ms) */
    31→  deletedAt: number;
    32→  /** File/folder size in bytes (0 for folders with unknown size) */
    33→  size: number;
    34→  /** MIME type (empty string for folders) */
    35→  mimeType: string;
    36→
    37→  // --- Item reference data (needed for restore and permanent delete) ---
    38→
    39→  /** For files: the preserved FilePointer from parent folder metadata */
    40→  filePointer?: FilePointer;
    41→  /** For folders: the preserved FolderEntry from parent folder metadata */
    42→  folderEntry?: FolderEntry;
    43→};
    44→
    45→/**
    46→ * The full recycle bin metadata.
    47→ *
    48→ * Encrypted as a single JSON blob with the user's publicKey via ECIES,
    49→ * then stored on IPFS and referenced by a dedicated IPNS name.
    50→ * Follows the same pattern as DeviceRegistry.
    51→ */
    52→export type RecycleBinMetadata = {
    53→  /** Schema version for future migrations */
    54→  version: 'v1';
    55→  /** Monotonically increasing update counter */
    56→  sequenceNumber: number;
    57→  /** Array of all bin entries */
    58→  entries: BinEntry[];
    59→};
    60→
