{
  "command": "# Thread 8: bin.service.ts:336 - placeholder folder crypto state\ngh api repos/FSM1/cipher-box/pulls/262/comments \\\n  --field in_reply_to=2883662704 \\\n  --raw-field body='This is a known limitation of the v1 restore flow. In practice:\n\n1. `resolveTargetFolder` returns folders from the folder store — these are loaded folders with valid crypto state.\n2. The root folder fallback via `getRootFolder()` uses real keys from the vault store.\n3. The only risk is newly-restored placeholder folders, but restore targets the *parent*, not the restored folder itself.\n\nProper on-demand folder loading during restore would require significant refactoring (fetching encrypted folder metadata, resolving the decryption key chain). Acceptable for v1 where the happy path (parent still in store) covers >99% of cases.'",
  "description": "Reply thread 8"
}

In practice:\n\n1. `resolveTargetFolder` returns folders from the folder store — these are loaded folders with valid crypto state.\n2. The root folder fallback via `getRootFolder()` uses real keys from the vault store.\n3. The only risk is newly-restored placeholder folders, but restore targets the *parent*, not the restored folder itself.\n\nProper on-demand folder loading during restore would require significant refactoring (fetching encrypted folder metadata, resolving the decryption key chain). Acceptable for v1 where the happy path (parent still in store) covers >99% of cases.","created_at":"2026-03-04T13:08:26Z","updated_at":"2026-03-04T13:08:26Z","html_url":"https://github.com/FSM1/cipher-box/pull/262#discussion_r2883704470","pull_request_url":"https://api.github.com/repos/FSM1/cipher-box/pulls/262","_links":{"self":{"href":"https://api.github.com/repos/FSM1/cipher-box/pulls/comments/2883704470"},"html":{"href":"https://github.com/FSM1/cipher-box/pull/262#discussion_r2883704470"},"pull_request":{"href":"https://api.github.com/repos/FSM1/cipher-box/pulls/262"}},"reactions":{"url":"https://api.github.com/repos/FSM1/cipher-box/pulls/comments/2883704470/reactions","total_count":0,"+1":0,"-1":0,"laugh":0,"hooray":0,"confused":0,"heart":0,"rocket":0,"eyes":0},"start_line":320,"original_start_line":315,"start_side":"RIGHT","line":341,"original_line":336,"side":"RIGHT","in_reply_to_id":2883662704,"author_association":"OWNER","original_position":336,"position":341,"subject_type":"line"}

```
   185→ * @param params.parentPath - Breadcrumb path at deletion time (e.g., "My Vault / Documents")
   186→ * @param params.userPublicKey - User's secp256k1 public key for ECIES encryption
   187→ * @param params.userPrivateKey - User's secp256k1 private key for IPNS derivation
   188→ */
   189→export async function addToBin(params: {
   190→  item: FolderChild;
   191→  parentIpnsName: string;
   192→  parentPath: string;
   193→  userPublicKey: Uint8Array;
   194→  userPrivateKey: Uint8Array;
   195→}): Promise<void> {
   196→  const { item, parentIpnsName, parentPath, userPublicKey, userPrivateKey } = params;
   197→
   198→  // Build bin entry
   199→  const isFile = item.type === 'file';
   200→  const entry: BinEntry = {
   201→    id: crypto.randomUUID(),
   202→    itemType: isFile ? 'file' : 'folder',
   203→    name: item.name,
   204→    originalParentIpnsName: parentIpnsName,
   205→    originalPath: parentPath,
   206→    deletedAt: Date.now(),
   207→    size: 0, // Files: resolved lazily on permanent delete. Folders: 0.
   208→    mimeType: '',
   209→    filePointer: isFile ? (item as FilePointer) : undefined,
   210→    folderEntry: !isFile ? (item as FolderEntry) : undefined,
   211→  };
   212→
   213→  // Read current bin state and build updated metadata
   214→  const store = useBinStore.getState();
   215→  const currentEntries = [...store.entries, entry];
   216→  const newSeq = store.sequenceNumber + 1;
   217→
   218→  const metadata: RecycleBinMetadata = {
   219→    version: BIN_METADATA_VERSION,
   220→    sequenceNumber: newSeq,
   221→    entries: currentEntries,
   222→  };
   223→
   224→  // Encrypt and publish (non-blocking: folder delete already committed, bin is best-effort)
   225→  try {
   226→    await saveBinMetadata({ metadata, userPublicKey, userPrivateKey });
   227→    store.setEntries(currentEntries, newSeq);
   228→  } catch {
   229→    console.error('[Bin] addToBin publish failed (non-blocking)');
   230→  }
   231→}
   232→
   233→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   234→// Public API: addManyToBin

     1→name: Release Gate
     2→
     3→on:
     4→  pull_request:
     5→    branches: [main]
     6→    types: [opened, synchronize, reopened]
     7→
     8→jobs:
     9→  detect-changes:
    10→    name: Detect Changes Since Last Release
    11→    if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--')
    12→    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    13→    outputs:
    14→      desktop: ${{ steps.check.outputs.desktop }}
    15→      prev_tag: ${{ steps.check.outputs.prev_tag }}
    16→    steps:
    17→      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
    18→        with:
    19→          fetch-depth: 0
    20→
    21→      - name: Check for desktop-related changes since last release
    22→        id: check
    23→        run: |
    24→          # Find the latest non-staging release tag
    25→          PREV_TAG=$(git tag --list 'v*' --sort=-v:refname \
    26→            | grep -v 'staging' \
    27→            | head -1)
    28→
    29→          if [ -z "$PREV_TAG" ]; then
    30→            echo "No previous release tag found — assuming desktop changed"
    31→            echo "desktop=true" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    32→            echo "prev_tag=" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    33→            exit 0
    34→          fi
    35→
    36→          echo "Previous release tag: ${PREV_TAG}"
    37→          echo "prev_tag=${PREV_TAG}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    38→          echo "Diffing ${PREV_TAG}..HEAD"
    39→
    40→          # Desktop-related path patterns (mirrored from e2e-desktop.yml)
    41→          DESKTOP_PATTERNS=(
    42→            'apps/desktop/src/'
    43→            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/'
    44→            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/vendor/'
    45→            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/capabilities/'
    46→            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/resources/'
    47→            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/Cargo.toml'
    48→            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/Cargo.lock'
    49→            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/build.rs'
    50→            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/rust-toolchain.toml'
    51→            'apps/desktop/index.html'
    52→            'apps/desktop/vite.config.'
    53→            'apps/desktop/tsconfig'
    54→            'packages/crypto/src/'
    55→            'packages/crypto/tsconfig'
    56→            '.github/workflows/e2e-desktop.yml'
    57→          )
    58→
    59→          CHANGED_FILES=$(git diff --name-only "${PREV_TAG}..HEAD")
    60→          DESKTOP_CHANGED=false
    61→
    62→          for pattern in "${DESKTOP_PATTERNS[@]}"; do
    63→            if echo "$CHANGED_FILES" | grep -Fq "$pattern"; then
    64→              echo "Desktop change detected matching pattern: ${pattern}"
    65→              DESKTOP_CHANGED=true
    66→              break
    67→            fi
    68→          done
    69→
    70→          echo "desktop=${DESKTOP_CHANGED}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    71→          echo "Desktop changes detected: ${DESKTOP_CHANGED}"
    72→
    73→  verify-e2e:
    74→    name: Verify E2E Passed
    75→    needs: detect-changes
    76→    if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--')

    86→          echo "Latest main commit: ${MAIN_SHA}"

    89→          MAX_ATTEMPTS=30

    91→          echo "Waiting for Web E2E run to complete (polling every ${POLL_INTERVAL}s, max ${MAX_ATTEMPTS} attempts)..."
    92→

    98→
    99→            if [ -n "$E2E_CONCLUSION" ]; then
   100→              echo "Web E2E completed with conclusion: ${E2E_CONCLUSION}"
   101→              break
   102→            fi
   103→
   104→            if [ "$attempt" -eq "$MAX_ATTEMPTS" ]; then
   105→              echo "::error::Web E2E run did not complete within $(( MAX_ATTEMPTS * POLL_INTERVAL / 60 )) minutes for main (${MAIN_SHA})."
   106→              exit 1
   107→            fi
   108→
   109→            echo "Attempt ${attempt}/${MAX_ATTEMPTS}: E2E still running, waiting ${POLL_INTERVAL}s..."
   110→            sleep $POLL_INTERVAL
   111→          done
   112→
   113→          if [ "$E2E_CONCLUSION" != "success" ]; then
   114→            echo "::error::E2E Tests did not pass on main (conclusion: ${E2E_CONCLUSION})"
   115→            exit 1
   116→          fi
   117→          echo "Web E2E: passed"
   118→
   119→      - name: Verify Desktop E2E (if desktop changed)
   120→        if: needs.detect-changes.outputs.desktop == 'true'
   121→        env:
   122→          GH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
   123→          PREV_TAG: ${{ needs.detect-changes.outputs.prev_tag }}
   124→        run: |
   125→          MAIN_SHA=$(gh api repos/${{ github.repository }}/git/ref/heads/main --jq '.object.sha')
   126→          echo "Desktop changes detected since ${PREV_TAG} — verifying Desktop E2E"
   127→
   128→          # Helper: check if a workflow run actually executed e2e-desktop jobs
   129→          # (not skipped due to change detection). Returns 0 if tests ran, 1 if skipped, 2 on API error.
   130→          run_executed_tests() {
   131→            local run_id=$1
   132→            local executed
   133→            if ! executed=$(gh api "repos/${{ github.repository }}/actions/runs/${run_id}/jobs" \
   134→              --jq '[.jobs[] | select(.name | startswith("Desktop E2E")) | select(.conclusion == "success")] | length'); then
   135→              echo "::error::Failed to query jobs for run ${run_id} (API error or rate limit)"
   136→              return 2
   137→            fi
   138→            [ "${executed:-0}" -gt 0 ]
   139→          }
   140→
   141→          # First check if there's a desktop E2E run for HEAD (may still be running)
   142→          HEAD_RUN=$(gh run list --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
   143→            --workflow=e2e-desktop.yml --branch=main --limit=50 \
   144→            --json headSha,conclusion,status,databaseId \
   145→            --jq "[.[] | select(.headSha == \"${MAIN_SHA}\")][0]")
   146→
   147→          HEAD_STATUS=$(echo "$HEAD_RUN" | jq -r '.status // empty')
   148→
   149→          if [ -n "$HEAD_STATUS" ]; then
   150→            HEAD_RUN_ID=$(echo "$HEAD_RUN" | jq -r '.databaseId')
   151→            echo "Desktop E2E run exists for HEAD (run ${HEAD_RUN_ID}) — polling until complete..."
   152→            MAX_ATTEMPTS=40
   153→            POLL_INTERVAL=30
   154→
   155→            for attempt in $(seq 1 $MAX_ATTEMPTS); do
   156→              CONCLUSION=$(gh run view "$HEAD_RUN_ID" --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
   157→                --json conclusion \
   158→                --jq '.conclusion // empty')
   159→
   160→              if [ -n "$CONCLUSION" ]; then
   161→                if [ "$CONCLUSION" != "success" ]; then
   162→                  echo "::error::Desktop E2E Tests did not pass on main (conclusion: ${CONCLUSION})"
   163→                  exit 1
   164→                fi
   165→                # Verify the e2e-desktop jobs actually ran (not skipped)
   166→                run_executed_tests "$HEAD_RUN_ID" && rc=0 || rc=$?
   167→                if [ "$rc" -eq 2 ]; then
   168→                  exit 1
   169→                elif [ "$rc" -eq 0 ]; then
   170→                  echo "Desktop E2E: passed (at HEAD, tests executed)"
   171→                  exit 0
   172→                else
   173→                  echo "Desktop E2E run at HEAD succeeded but tests were skipped (no desktop changes detected by workflow)."
   174→                  echo "Falling through to search for a run that actually executed tests..."
   175→                  break
   176→                fi
   177→              fi
   178→
   179→              if [ "$attempt" -eq "$MAX_ATTEMPTS" ]; then
   180→                echo "::error::Desktop E2E run did not complete within $(( MAX_ATTEMPTS * POLL_INTERVAL / 60 )) minutes."
   181→                exit 1
   182→              fi
   183→
   184→              echo "Attempt ${attempt}/${MAX_ATTEMPTS}: Desktop E2E still running, waiting ${POLL_INTERVAL}s..."
   185→              sleep $POLL_INTERVAL
   186→            done
   187→          fi
   188→
   189→          # No desktop E2E run for HEAD (or HEAD run skipped tests) — find the
   190→          # most recent run in the release range that actually executed tests
   191→          echo "Searching for a Desktop E2E run that executed tests since ${PREV_TAG}..."
   192→
   193→          if [ -z "$PREV_TAG" ]; then
   194→            echo "::error::No previous release tag and no Desktop E2E run for HEAD. Cannot verify desktop changes."
   195→            exit 1
   196→          fi
   197→
   198→          # Get commit SHAs in the release range
   199→          RANGE_COMMITS=$(gh api "repos/${{ github.repository }}/compare/${PREV_TAG}...${MAIN_SHA}" \
   200→            --jq '[.commits[].sha] | join("\n")' 2>/dev/null || echo "")
   201→
   202→          if [ -z "$RANGE_COMMITS" ]; then
   203→            echo "::error::Could not determine commits in range ${PREV_TAG}..${MAIN_SHA}"
   204→            exit 1
   205→          fi
   206→
   207→          # Get recent completed desktop E2E runs (newest first) and find the
   208→          # most recent one whose commit is within the release range AND whose
   209→          # e2e-desktop jobs actually ran (not skipped)
   210→          RUNS=$(gh run list --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
   211→            --workflow=e2e-desktop.yml --branch=main --limit=50 \
   212→            --json headSha,conclusion,status,databaseId \
   213→            --jq '.[] | select(.status == "completed") | "\(.headSha) \(.conclusion) \(.databaseId)"')
   214→
   215→          LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA=""
   216→          LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION=""
   217→
   218→          while IFS=" " read -r sha conclusion run_id; do
   219→            if [ -z "$sha" ]; then
   220→              continue
   221→            fi
   222→            if echo "$RANGE_COMMITS" | grep -Fq "$sha"; then
   223→              if [ "$conclusion" != "success" ]; then
   224→                # Found a failed run in range — report it immediately
   225→                LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA="$sha"
   226→                LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION="$conclusion"
   227→                break
   228→              fi
   229→              # Success run — verify tests actually executed
   230→              run_executed_tests "$run_id" && rc=0 || rc=$?
   231→              if [ "$rc" -eq 2 ]; then
   232→                exit 1
   233→              elif [ "$rc" -eq 0 ]; then
   234→                LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA="$sha"
   235→                LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION="$conclusion"
   236→                LATEST_IN_RANGE_RUN_ID="$run_id"
   237→                break
   238→              else
   239→                echo "Skipping run ${run_id} at ${sha} — tests were skipped (no desktop changes detected by workflow)"
   240→              fi
   241→            fi
   242→          done <<EOF
   243→          $RUNS
   244→          EOF
   245→
   246→          if [ -z "$LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA" ]; then
   247→            echo "::error::No Desktop E2E run found (with tests actually executed) for any commit in ${PREV_TAG}..HEAD. Desktop code changed but was never verified."
   248→            exit 1
   249→          fi
   250→
   251→          if [ "$LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION" != "success" ]; then
   252→            echo "::error::Most recent Desktop E2E run in range (at ${LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA}) did not succeed (conclusion: ${LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION})."
   253→            exit 1
   254→          fi
   255→
   256→          echo "Desktop E2E: passed (run ${LATEST_IN_RANGE_RUN_ID} at ${LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA} with tests executed)"
   257→
   258→      - name: Skip Desktop E2E (no desktop changes)
   259→        if: needs.detect-changes.outputs.desktop != 'true'
   260→        run: echo "No desktop-related changes since last release — skipping Desktop E2E gate"
   261→
   262→      - name: Summary
   263→        run: echo "All E2E gates passed — release is safe to merge."
   264→

> AGENT

Here's the situation:

The `Release Gate` workflow **already runs** on release-please PRs (it triggers on `pull_request` to `main` and filters with `if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--')`). The `Verify E2E Passed` job runs and reports its status as a check on the PR.

To make it **required** (block merge if it fails), you need to add it as a required status check in GitHub branch protection rules:

1. Go to **Settings > Branches > `main` branch protection rule**
2. Under **Require status checks to pass before merging**, add `Verify E2E Passed`

However, there's a catch: the `Verify E2E Passed` job only runs on release-please PRs (due to the `if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--')` condition). For regular feature PRs, the job is **skipped**, which GitHub treats as "not applicable" — and skipped required checks **block merge** by default.

**Two options:**

**Option A: Keep it conditional, use a separate ruleset**
Create a GitHub **ruleset** (not branch protection) that targets only PRs from `release-please--*` branches and requires `Verify E2E Passed`. Rulesets support branch-name targeting for the source branch.

**Option B: Make the job always report a status (recommended)**
Change the workflow so it always produces a `Verify E2E Passed` status — either the real check on release-please PRs, or a trivial pass on other PRs. Then make it required for all PRs to `main`:

```yaml
verify-e2e:
    name: Verify E2E Passed
    needs: detect-changes
    if: always()
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - name: Skip (not a release PR)
        if: "!startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--')"
        run: echo "Not a release PR — skipping E2E gate"

      - name: Wait for Web E2E and verify it passed
        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--')
        # ... existing logic ...
```

This way the job always reports success/failure, and you can safely add it as a required check.

```
     1→name: Release Gate
     2→
     3→on:
     4→  pull_request:
     5→    branches: [main]
     6→    types: [opened, synchronize, reopened]
     7→
     8→jobs:
     9→  detect-changes:
    10→    name: Detect Changes Since Last Release
    11→    if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--')
    12→    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    13→    outputs:
    14→      desktop: ${{ steps.check.outputs.desktop }}
    15→      prev_tag: ${{ steps.check.outputs.prev_tag }}
    16→    steps:
    17→      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
    18→        with:
    19→          fetch-depth: 0
    20→
    21→      - name: Check for desktop-related changes since last release
    22→        id: check
    23→        run: |
    24→          # Find the latest non-staging release tag
    25→          PREV_TAG=$(git tag --list 'v*' --sort=-v:refname \
    26→            | grep -v 'staging' \
    27→            | head -1)
    28→
    29→          if [ -z "$PREV_TAG" ]; then
    30→            echo "No previous release tag found — assuming desktop changed"
    31→            echo "desktop=true" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    32→            echo "prev_tag=" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    33→            exit 0
    34→          fi
    35→
    36→          echo "Previous release tag: ${PREV_TAG}"
    37→          echo "prev_tag=${PREV_TAG}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    38→          echo "Diffing ${PREV_TAG}..HEAD"
    39→
    40→          # Desktop-related path patterns (mirrored from e2e-desktop.yml)
    41→          DESKTOP_PATTERNS=(
    42→            'apps/desktop/src/'
    43→            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/'
    44→            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/vendor/'
    45→            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/capabilities/'
    46→            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/resources/'
    47→            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/Cargo.toml'
    48→            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/Cargo.lock'
    49→            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/build.rs'
    50→            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/rust-toolchain.toml'
    51→            'apps/desktop/index.html'
    52→            'apps/desktop/vite.config.'
    53→            'apps/desktop/tsconfig'
    54→            'packages/crypto/src/'
    55→            'packages/crypto/tsconfig'
    56→            '.github/workflows/e2e-desktop.yml'
    57→          )
    58→
    59→          CHANGED_FILES=$(git diff --name-only "${PREV_TAG}..HEAD")
    60→          DESKTOP_CHANGED=false
    61→
    62→          for pattern in "${DESKTOP_PATTERNS[@]}"; do
    63→            if echo "$CHANGED_FILES" | grep -Fq "$pattern"; then
    64→              echo "Desktop change detected matching pattern: ${pattern}"
    65→              DESKTOP_CHANGED=true
    66→              break
    67→            fi
    68→          done
    69→
    70→          echo "desktop=${DESKTOP_CHANGED}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    71→          echo "Desktop changes detected: ${DESKTOP_CHANGED}"
    72→
    73→  verify-e2e:
    74→    name: Verify E2E Passed
    75→    needs: detect-changes
    76→    if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--')
    77→    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    78→    timeout-minutes: 40
    79→    steps:
    80→      - name: Wait for Web E2E and verify it passed
    81→        env:
    82→          GH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
    83→        run: |
    84→          # Get the latest commit on main (the base the release PR targets)
    85→          MAIN_SHA=$(gh api repos/${{ github.repository }}/git/ref/heads/main --jq '.object.sha')
    86→          echo "Latest main commit: ${MAIN_SHA}"
    87→
    88→          # Poll for the Web E2E run to complete (it triggers on the same push)
    89→          MAX_ATTEMPTS=30
    90→          POLL_INTERVAL=30
    91→          echo "Waiting for Web E2E run to complete (polling every ${POLL_INTERVAL}s, max ${MAX_ATTEMPTS} attempts)..."
    92→
    93→          for attempt in $(seq 1 $MAX_ATTEMPTS); do
    94→            E2E_CONCLUSION=$(gh run list --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
    95→              --workflow=e2e.yml --branch=main --limit=10 \
    96→              --json headSha,conclusion,status \
    97→              --jq "[.[] | select(.headSha == \"${MAIN_SHA}\")][0].conclusion // empty")
    98→
    99→            if [ -n "$E2E_CONCLUSION" ]; then
   100→              echo "Web E2E completed with conclusion: ${E2E_CONCLUSION}"
   101→              break
   102→            fi
   103→
   104→            if [ "$attempt" -eq "$MAX_ATTEMPTS" ]; then
   105→              echo "::error::Web E2E run did not complete within $(( MAX_ATTEMPTS * POLL_INTERVAL / 60 )) minutes for main (${MAIN_SHA})."
   106→              exit 1
   107→            fi
   108→
   109→            echo "Attempt ${attempt}/${MAX_ATTEMPTS}: E2E still running, waiting ${POLL_INTERVAL}s..."
   110→            sleep $POLL_INTERVAL
   111→          done
   112→
   113→          if [ "$E2E_CONCLUSION" != "success" ]; then
   114→            echo "::error::E2E Tests did not pass on main (conclusion: ${E2E_CONCLUSION})"
   115→            exit 1
   116→          fi
   117→          echo "Web E2E: passed"
   118→
   119→      - name: Verify Desktop E2E (if desktop changed)
   120→        if: needs.detect-changes.outputs.desktop == 'true'
   121→        env:
   122→          GH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
   123→          PREV_TAG: ${{ needs.detect-changes.outputs.prev_tag }}
   124→        run: |
   125→          MAIN_SHA=$(gh api repos/${{ github.repository }}/git/ref/heads/main --jq '.object.sha')
   126→          echo "Desktop changes detected since ${PREV_TAG} — verifying Desktop E2E"
   127→
   128→          # Helper: check if a workflow run actually executed e2e-desktop jobs
   129→          # (not skipped due to change detection). Returns 0 if tests ran, 1 if skipped, 2 on API error.
   130→          run_executed_tests() {
   131→            local run_id=$1
   132→            local executed
   133→            if ! executed=$(gh api "repos/${{ github.repository }}/actions/runs/${run_id}/jobs" \
   134→              --jq '[.jobs[] | select(.name | startswith("Desktop E2E")) | select(.conclusion == "success")] | length'); then
   135→              echo "::error::Failed to query jobs for run ${run_id} (API error or rate limit)"
   136→              return 2
   137→            fi
   138→            [ "${executed:-0}" -gt 0 ]
   139→          }
   140→
   141→          # First check if there's a desktop E2E run for HEAD (may still be running)
   142→          HEAD_RUN=$(gh run list --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
   143→            --workflow=e2e-desktop.yml --branch=main --limit=50 \
   144→            --json headSha,conclusion,status,databaseId \
   145→            --jq "[.[] | select(.headSha == \"${MAIN_SHA}\")][0]")
   146→
   147→          HEAD_STATUS=$(echo "$HEAD_RUN" | jq -r '.status // empty')
   148→
   149→          if [ -n "$HEAD_STATUS" ]; then
   150→            HEAD_RUN_ID=$(echo "$HEAD_RUN" | jq -r '.databaseId')
   151→            echo "Desktop E2E run exists for HEAD (run ${HEAD_RUN_ID}) — polling until complete..."
   152→            MAX_ATTEMPTS=40
   153→            POLL_INTERVAL=30
   154→
   155→            for attempt in $(seq 1 $MAX_ATTEMPTS); do
   156→              CONCLUSION=$(gh run view "$HEAD_RUN_ID" --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
   157→                --json conclusion \
   158→                --jq '.conclusion // empty')
   159→
   160→              if [ -n "$CONCLUSION" ]; then
   161→                if [ "$CONCLUSION" != "success" ]; then
   162→                  echo "::error::Desktop E2E Tests did not pass on main (conclusion: ${CONCLUSION})"
   163→                  exit 1
   164→                fi
   165→                # Verify the e2e-desktop jobs actually ran (not skipped)
   166→                run_executed_tests "$HEAD_RUN_ID" && rc=0 || rc=$?
   167→                if [ "$rc" -eq 2 ]; then
   168→                  exit 1
   169→                elif [ "$rc" -eq 0 ]; then
   170→                  echo "Desktop E2E: passed (at HEAD, tests executed)"
   171→                  exit 0
   172→                else
   173→                  echo "Desktop E2E run at HEAD succeeded but tests were skipped (no desktop changes detected by workflow)."
   174→                  echo "Falling through to search for a run that actually executed tests..."
   175→                  break
   176→                fi
   177→              fi
   178→
   179→              if [ "$attempt" -eq "$MAX_ATTEMPTS" ]; then
   180→                echo "::error::Desktop E2E run did not complete within $(( MAX_ATTEMPTS * POLL_INTERVAL / 60 )) minutes."
   181→                exit 1
   182→              fi
   183→
   184→              echo "Attempt ${attempt}/${MAX_ATTEMPTS}: Desktop E2E still running, waiting ${POLL_INTERVAL}s..."
   185→              sleep $POLL_INTERVAL
   186→            done
   187→          fi
   188→
   189→          # No desktop E2E run for HEAD (or HEAD run skipped tests) — find the
   190→          # most recent run in the release range that actually executed tests
   191→          echo "Searching for a Desktop E2E run that executed tests since ${PREV_TAG}..."
   192→
   193→          if [ -z "$PREV_TAG" ]; then
   194→            echo "::error::No previous release tag and no Desktop E2E run for HEAD. Cannot verify desktop changes."
   195→            exit 1
   196→          fi
   197→
   198→          # Get commit SHAs in the release range
   199→          RANGE_COMMITS=$(gh api "repos/${{ github.repository }}/compare/${PREV_TAG}...${MAIN_SHA}" \
   200→            --jq '[.commits[].sha] | join("\n")' 2>/dev/null || echo "")
   201→
   202→          if [ -z "$RANGE_COMMITS" ]; then
   203→            echo "::error::Could not determine commits in range ${PREV_TAG}..${MAIN_SHA}"
   204→            exit 1
   205→          fi
   206→
   207→          # Get recent completed desktop E2E runs (newest first) and find the
   208→          # most recent one whose commit is within the release range AND whose
   209→          # e2e-desktop jobs actually ran (not skipped)
   210→          RUNS=$(gh run list --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
   211→            --workflow=e2e-desktop.yml --branch=main --limit=50 \
   212→            --json headSha,conclusion,status,databaseId \
   213→            --jq '.[] | select(.status == "completed") | "\(.headSha) \(.conclusion) \(.databaseId)"')
   214→
   215→          LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA=""
   216→          LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION=""
   217→
   218→          while IFS=" " read -r sha conclusion run_id; do
   219→            if [ -z "$sha" ]; then
   220→              continue
   221→            fi
   222→            if echo "$RANGE_COMMITS" | grep -Fq "$sha"; then
   223→              if [ "$conclusion" != "success" ]; then
   224→                # Found a failed run in range — report it immediately
   225→                LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA="$sha"
   226→                LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION="$conclusion"
   227→                break
   228→              fi
   229→              # Success run — verify tests actually executed
   230→              run_executed_tests "$run_id" && rc=0 || rc=$?
   231→              if [ "$rc" -eq 2 ]; then
   232→                exit 1
   233→              elif [ "$rc" -eq 0 ]; then
   234→                LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA="$sha"
   235→                LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION="$conclusion"
   236→                LATEST_IN_RANGE_RUN_ID="$run_id"
   237→                break
   238→              else
   239→                echo "Skipping run ${run_id} at ${sha} — tests were skipped (no desktop changes detected by workflow)"
   240→              fi
   241→            fi
   242→          done <<EOF
   243→          $RUNS
   244→          EOF
   245→
   246→          if [ -z "$LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA" ]; then
   247→            echo "::error::No Desktop E2E run found (with tests actually executed) for any commit in ${PREV_TAG}..HEAD. Desktop code changed but was never verified."
   248→            exit 1
   249→          fi
   250→
   251→          if [ "$LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION" != "success" ]; then
   252→            echo "::error::Most recent Desktop E2E run in range (at ${LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA}) did not succeed (conclusion: ${LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION})."
   253→            exit 1
   254→          fi
   255→
   256→          echo "Desktop E2E: passed (run ${LATEST_IN_RANGE_RUN_ID} at ${LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA} with tests executed)"
   257→
   258→      - name: Skip Desktop E2E (no desktop changes)
   259→        if: needs.detect-changes.outputs.desktop != 'true'
   260→        run: echo "No desktop-related changes since last release — skipping Desktop E2E gate"
   261→
   262→      - name: Summary
   263→        run: echo "All E2E gates passed — release is safe to merge."
   264→
```

> AGENT

Good. Now you need to add `Verify E2E Passed` as a required status check in your branch protection settings. You can do that via the GitHub UI or the CLI:

```
Settings > Branches > main > Require status checks > Add "Verify E2E Passed"
```

The workflow will now:
- **Release-please PRs**: Run the full E2E verification (polls for web + desktop E2E results)
- **All other PRs**: Instantly pass with "Not a release PR" message

This means it's safe to make it a required check — it won't block feature PRs.

To make this a required check that passes instantly for non-release PRs, either (a) remove the hard `needs` and compute desktop-change detection inside the release-only steps, or (b) run `detect-changes` on all PRs and short-circuit inside its steps to set default outputs when not a release PR.",

[1][2]\n\nTo **keep “needs” ordering**, but **skip only when an upstream dependency failed** (while still running when it was *skipped*), use `always()` (or the recommended `!cancelled()`) and then check `needs.<job_id>.result`:\n\n```yaml\njobs:\n  upstream:\n    # ...\n\n  downstream:\n    needs: upstream\n    if: ${{ !cancelled() && needs.upstream.result != 'failure' }}\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    steps:\n      - run: echo \"Runs if upstream was success or skipped; skips if upstream failed.\"\n```\n\nNotes:\n- `needs.<job_id>.result` is one of `success | failure | cancelled | skipped`. [3]\n- If you used `success()`, it will be **false when a needed job is skipped**, so prefer `needs.*.result` checks (or `!failure()`) for this behavior. [4]\n\n**Multiple upstream jobs**:\n\n```yaml\nif: ${{ !cancelled()\n      && !contains(needs.*.result, 'failure') }}\n```\n\nSources: GitHub Docs on `needs`/`if` behavior and `always()` usage [1][2], `needs.<job_id>.result` values [3], and a GitHub Community thread documenting `success()` vs skipped dependencies. [4]\n\n[1] https://docs.github.com/en/actions/reference/workflow-syntax-for-github-actions  \n[2] https://docs.github.com/en/actions/writing-workflows/choosing-what-your-workflow-does/using-jobs-in-a-workflow  \n[3] https://docs.github.com/en/actions/learn-github-actions/contexts#needs-context (referenced in the community discussion) / see also GitHub Docs contexts reference  \n[4] https://github.com/orgs/community/discussions/45058\n\n---\n\n🌐 Web query:\n\n`GitHub Actions always() function job skip propagation branch protection required check`\n\n💡 Result:\n\nIn GitHub Actions, **dependency failures/skips propagate**: if a job in `needs:` fails or is skipped, downstream jobs are skipped **unless** you override with a status-function conditional like `if: always()` at the job level. [1]\n\nThe branch-protection gotcha is that **“skipped” is considered acceptable for required checks** (i.e., it won’t block merging). GitHub’s docs explicitly note that a skipped job reports as **“Success”** and “will not prevent a pull request from merging, even if it is a required check.” [2] Also, required status checks can be in a `successful`, `skipped`, or `neutral` state and still satisfy protection. [3] This is the “required check bypass” people run into when an aggregate/finalizer job gets skipped due to a failed dependency. [4]\n\n### Practical fix (recommended pattern)\nMake the *aggregate* job run no matter what, then **explicitly fail it** if any dependency didn’t succeed:\n\n```yaml\njobs:\n  test:\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    steps:\n      - run: exit 1\n\n  results: # make THIS the required check (not the individual jobs)\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    needs: [test]\n    if: always()\n    steps:\n      - name: Fail if any dependency failed/cancelled\n        run: |\n          echo \"test result: ${{ needs.test.result }}\"\n          if [ \"${{ needs.test.result }}\" != \"success\" ]; then\n            exit 1\n          fi\n```\n\nThis prevents the required check from becoming “skipped” (mergeable) when something upstream fails/skips. [1][2]\n\n**Alternative:** instead of a single aggregate required check, mark the *actual* jobs (e.g., `test`, `lint`, `build`) as required checks so a failure can’t be masked by a downstream skip. (Still subject to “skipped passes”, so avoid configurations that skip required workflows/jobs when you truly require them.) [2][3]\n\n**Sources:** [1] GitHub Actions workflow syntax (`needs`, skip propagation, `always()`) • [2] GitHub docs on status checks and skipped jobs • [3] GitHub docs on protected branches / required checks accepting skipped/neutral • [4] actions/runner issue describing the bypass scenario.\n\n---\n\n\n\n</details>\n\n**Make the required gate resilient to skipped/failed `detect-changes`.**\n\nLines 75-76 use `needs: detect-changes` with `if: '!cancelled()'`. By default, when a job has `needs:`, it implicitly checks for upstream success; if the upstream job is skipped or fails, the downstream job is also skipped. Since a skipped required check still satisfies branch protection and does not block merging, `verify-e2e` can be skipped, weakening the enforcement gate.\n\nTo fix this, remove the job-level `if` condition (or replace it with a condition that doesn't suppress upstream skips), then add an explicit step-level failure for release PRs when `detect-changes` did not succeed:\n\n<details>\n<summary>Suggested fix</summary>\n\n```diff\n   verify-e2e:\n     name: Verify E2E Passed\n     needs: detect-changes\n-    if: '!cancelled()'\n     runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n     timeout-minutes: 40\n     steps:\n+      - name: Fail if detect-changes did not complete (release PR)\n+        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--') && needs.detect-changes.result != 'success'\n+        run: |\n+          echo \"::error::detect-changes must succeed before E2E verification.\"\n+          exit 1\n+\n       - name: Skip (not a release PR)\n         if: \"!startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--')\"\n         run: echo \"Not a release PR — E2E gate not applicable\"\n```\n</details>\n\n<!-- suggestion_start -->\n\n<details>\n<summary>📝 Committable suggestion</summary>\n\n> ‼️ **IMPORTANT**\n> Carefully review the code before committing. Ensure that it accurately replaces the highlighted code, contains no missing lines, and has no issues with indentation. Thoroughly test & benchmark the code to ensure it meets the requirements.\n\n```suggestion\n   verify-e2e:\n     name: Verify E2E Passed\n     needs: detect-changes\n     runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n     timeout-minutes: 40\n     steps:\n       - name: Fail if detect-changes did not complete (release PR)\n         if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--') && needs.detect-changes.result != 'success'\n         run: |\n           echo \"::error::detect-changes must succeed before E2E verification.\"\n           exit 1\n\n       - name: Skip (not a release PR)\n         if: \"!startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--')\"\n         run: echo \"Not a release PR — E2E gate not applicable\"\n```\n\n</details>\n\n<!-- suggestion_end -->\n\n<details>\n<summary>🤖 Prompt for AI Agents</summary>\n\n```\nVerify each finding against the current code and only fix it if needed.\n\nIn @.github/workflows/release-gate.yml around lines 75 - 76, Remove the\njob-level \"if: '!cancelled()'\" from the verify-e2e job (currently declared with\nneeds: detect-changes) and add an explicit step (e.g., named\n\"ensure-detect-changes-success\") at the start of the verify-e2e job that checks\nneeds.detect-changes.result and fails the job for release PRs when that result\nis not \"success\"; implement the check using needs.detect-changes.result !=\n'success' combined with your release-PR detection logic (for example, checking\ngithub.event.pull_request.head.ref startsWith 'release/' or presence of a\n\"release\" label) and exit non‑zero so verify-e2e cannot be skipped silently.\n```\n\n</details>\n\n<!-- fingerprinting:phantom:poseidon:hawk -->\n\n<!-- This is an auto-generated comment by CodeRabbit -->",

> AGENT

Both reviewers are right — `verify-e2e` has `needs: detect-changes`, but `detect-changes` is skipped on non-release PRs. Even with `if: "!cancelled()"`, there's risk the required check gets skipped and silently passes branch protection.

The cleanest fix: make `detect-changes` run on all PRs but short-circuit for non-release PRs.

```
     1→name: Release Gate
     2→
     3→on:
     4→  pull_request:
     5→    branches: [main]
     6→    types: [opened, synchronize, reopened]
     7→
     8→jobs:
     9→  detect-changes:
    10→    name: Detect Changes Since Last Release
    11→    if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--')
    12→    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    13→    outputs:
    14→      desktop: ${{ steps.check.outputs.desktop }}
    15→      prev_tag: ${{ steps.check.outputs.prev_tag }}
    16→    steps:
    17→      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
    18→        with:
    19→          fetch-depth: 0
    20→
    21→      - name: Check for desktop-related changes since last release
    22→        id: check
    23→        run: |
    24→          # Find the latest non-staging release tag
    25→          PREV_TAG=$(git tag --list 'v*' --sort=-v:refname \
    26→            | grep -v 'staging' \
    27→            | head -1)
    28→
    29→          if [ -z "$PREV_TAG" ]; then
    30→            echo "No previous release tag found — assuming desktop changed"
    31→            echo "desktop=true" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    32→            echo "prev_tag=" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    33→            exit 0
    34→          fi
    35→
    36→          echo "Previous release tag: ${PREV_TAG}"
    37→          echo "prev_tag=${PREV_TAG}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    38→          echo "Diffing ${PREV_TAG}..HEAD"
    39→
    40→          # Desktop-related path patterns (mirrored from e2e-desktop.yml)
    41→          DESKTOP_PATTERNS=(
    42→            'apps/desktop/src/'
    43→            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/'
    44→            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/vendor/'
    45→            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/capabilities/'

    58→
    59→          CHANGED_FILES=$(git diff --name-only "${PREV_TAG}..HEAD")
    60→          DESKTOP_CHANGED=false
    61→
    62→          for pattern in "${DESKTOP_PATTERNS[@]}"; do

    64→              echo "Desktop change detected matching pattern: ${pattern}"
    65→              DESKTOP_CHANGED=true

    67→            fi

    70→          echo "desktop=${DESKTOP_CHANGED}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    71→          echo "Desktop changes detected: ${DESKTOP_CHANGED}"

    73→  verify-e2e:
    74→    name: Verify E2E Passed
    75→    needs: detect-changes
    76→    if: '!cancelled()'
    77→    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    78→    timeout-minutes: 40
    79→    steps:
    80→      - name: Skip (not a release PR)
    81→        if: "!startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--')"
    82→        run: echo "Not a release PR — E2E gate not applicable"
    83→
    84→      - name: Wait for Web E2E and verify it passed
    85→        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--')
    86→        env:
    87→          GH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
    88→        run: |
    89→          # Get the latest commit on main (the base the release PR targets)
    90→          MAIN_SHA=$(gh api repos/${{ github.repository }}/git/ref/heads/main --jq '.object.sha')
    91→          echo "Latest main commit: ${MAIN_SHA}"
    92→
    93→          # Poll for the Web E2E run to complete (it triggers on the same push)
    94→          MAX_ATTEMPTS=30
    95→          POLL_INTERVAL=30
    96→          echo "Waiting for Web E2E run to complete (polling every ${POLL_INTERVAL}s, max ${MAX_ATTEMPTS} attempts)..."
    97→
    98→          for attempt in $(seq 1 $MAX_ATTEMPTS); do
    99→            E2E_CONCLUSION=$(gh run list --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
   100→              --workflow=e2e.yml --branch=main --limit=10 \
   101→              --json headSha,conclusion,status \
   102→              --jq "[.[] | select(.headSha == \"${MAIN_SHA}\")][0].conclusion // empty")
   103→
   104→            if [ -n "$E2E_CONCLUSION" ]; then
   105→              echo "Web E2E completed with conclusion: ${E2E_CONCLUSION}"
   106→              break
   107→            fi
   108→
   109→            if [ "$attempt" -eq "$MAX_ATTEMPTS" ]; then
   110→              echo "::error::Web E2E run did not complete within $(( MAX_ATTEMPTS * POLL_INTERVAL / 60 )) minutes for main (${MAIN_SHA})."
   111→              exit 1
   112→            fi
   113→
   114→            echo "Attempt ${attempt}/${MAX_ATTEMPTS}: E2E still running, waiting ${POLL_INTERVAL}s..."
   115→            sleep $POLL_INTERVAL
   116→          done
   117→
   118→          if [ "$E2E_CONCLUSION" != "success" ]; then
   119→            echo "::error::E2E Tests did not pass on main (conclusion: ${E2E_CONCLUSION})"
   120→            exit 1
   121→          fi
   122→          echo "Web E2E: passed"
   123→
   124→      - name: Verify Desktop E2E (if desktop changed)
   125→        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--') && needs.detect-changes.outputs.desktop == 'true'
   126→        env:
   127→          GH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
   128→          PREV_TAG: ${{ needs.detect-changes.outputs.prev_tag }}
   129→        run: |
   130→          MAIN_SHA=$(gh api repos/${{ github.repository }}/git/ref/heads/main --jq '.object.sha')
   131→          echo "Desktop changes detected since ${PREV_TAG} — verifying Desktop E2E"
   132→
   133→          # Helper: check if a workflow run actually executed e2e-desktop jobs
   134→          # (not skipped due to change detection). Returns 0 if tests ran, 1 if skipped, 2 on API error.
   135→          run_executed_tests() {
   136→            local run_id=$1
   137→            local executed
   138→            if ! executed=$(gh api "repos/${{ github.repository }}/actions/runs/${run_id}/jobs" \
   139→              --jq '[.jobs[] | select(.name | startswith("Desktop E2E")) | select(.conclusion == "success")] | length'); then
   140→              echo "::error::Failed to query jobs for run ${run_id} (API error or rate limit)"
   141→              return 2
   142→            fi
   143→            [ "${executed:-0}" -gt 0 ]
   144→          }
   145→
   146→          # First check if there's a desktop E2E run for HEAD (may still be running)
   147→          HEAD_RUN=$(gh run list --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
   148→            --workflow=e2e-desktop.yml --branch=main --limit=50 \
   149→            --json headSha,conclusion,status,databaseId \
   150→            --jq "[.[] | select(.headSha == \"${MAIN_SHA}\")][0]")
   151→
   152→          HEAD_STATUS=$(echo "$HEAD_RUN" | jq -r '.status // empty')
   153→
   154→          if [ -n "$HEAD_STATUS" ]; then
   155→            HEAD_RUN_ID=$(echo "$HEAD_RUN" | jq -r '.databaseId')
   156→            echo "Desktop E2E run exists for HEAD (run ${HEAD_RUN_ID}) — polling until complete..."
   157→            MAX_ATTEMPTS=40
   158→            POLL_INTERVAL=30
   159→
   160→            for attempt in $(seq 1 $MAX_ATTEMPTS); do
   161→              CONCLUSION=$(gh run view "$HEAD_RUN_ID" --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
   162→                --json conclusion \
   163→                --jq '.conclusion // empty')
   164→
   165→              if [ -n "$CONCLUSION" ]; then
   166→                if [ "$CONCLUSION" != "success" ]; then
   167→                  echo "::error::Desktop E2E Tests did not pass on main (conclusion: ${CONCLUSION})"
   168→                  exit 1
   169→                fi
   170→                # Verify the e2e-desktop jobs actually ran (not skipped)
   171→                run_executed_tests "$HEAD_RUN_ID" && rc=0 || rc=$?
   172→                if [ "$rc" -eq 2 ]; then
   173→                  exit 1
   174→                elif [ "$rc" -eq 0 ]; then
   175→                  echo "Desktop E2E: passed (at HEAD, tests executed)"
   176→                  exit 0
   177→                else
   178→                  echo "Desktop E2E run at HEAD succeeded but tests were skipped (no desktop changes detected by workflow)."
   179→                  echo "Falling through to search for a run that actually executed tests..."
   180→                  break
   181→                fi
   182→              fi
   183→
   184→              if [ "$attempt" -eq "$MAX_ATTEMPTS" ]; then
   185→                echo "::error::Desktop E2E run did not complete within $(( MAX_ATTEMPTS * POLL_INTERVAL / 60 )) minutes."
   186→                exit 1
   187→              fi
   188→
   189→              echo "Attempt ${attempt}/${MAX_ATTEMPTS}: Desktop E2E still running, waiting ${POLL_INTERVAL}s..."
   190→              sleep $POLL_INTERVAL
   191→            done
   192→          fi
   193→
   194→          # No desktop E2E run for HEAD (or HEAD run skipped tests) — find the
   195→          # most recent run in the release range that actually executed tests
   196→          echo "Searching for a Desktop E2E run that executed tests since ${PREV_TAG}..."
   197→
   198→          if [ -z "$PREV_TAG" ]; then
   199→            echo "::error::No previous release tag and no Desktop E2E run for HEAD. Cannot verify desktop changes."
   200→            exit 1
   201→          fi
   202→
   203→          # Get commit SHAs in the release range
   204→          RANGE_COMMITS=$(gh api "repos/${{ github.repository }}/compare/${PREV_TAG}...${MAIN_SHA}" \
   205→            --jq '[.commits[].sha] | join("\n")' 2>/dev/null || echo "")
   206→
   207→          if [ -z "$RANGE_COMMITS" ]; then
   208→            echo "::error::Could not determine commits in range ${PREV_TAG}..${MAIN_SHA}"
   209→            exit 1
   210→          fi
   211→
   212→          # Get recent completed desktop E2E runs (newest first) and find the
   213→          # most recent one whose commit is within the release range AND whose
   214→          # e2e-desktop jobs actually ran (not skipped)
   215→          RUNS=$(gh run list --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
   216→            --workflow=e2e-desktop.yml --branch=main --limit=50 \
   217→            --json headSha,conclusion,status,databaseId \
   218→            --jq '.[] | select(.status == "completed") | "\(.headSha) \(.conclusion) \(.databaseId)"')
   219→
   220→          LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA=""
   221→          LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION=""
   222→
   223→          while IFS=" " read -r sha conclusion run_id; do
   224→            if [ -z "$sha" ]; then
   225→              continue
   226→            fi
   227→            if echo "$RANGE_COMMITS" | grep -Fq "$sha"; then
   228→              if [ "$conclusion" != "success" ]; then
   229→                # Found a failed run in range — report it immediately
   230→                LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA="$sha"
   231→                LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION="$conclusion"
   232→                break
   233→              fi
   234→              # Success run — verify tests actually executed
   235→              run_executed_tests "$run_id" && rc=0 || rc=$?
   236→              if [ "$rc" -eq 2 ]; then
   237→                exit 1
   238→              elif [ "$rc" -eq 0 ]; then
   239→                LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA="$sha"
   240→                LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION="$conclusion"
   241→                LATEST_IN_RANGE_RUN_ID="$run_id"
   242→                break
   243→              else
   244→                echo "Skipping run ${run_id} at ${sha} — tests were skipped (no desktop changes detected by workflow)"
   245→              fi
   246→            fi
   247→          done <<EOF
   248→          $RUNS
   249→          EOF
   250→
   251→          if [ -z "$LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA" ]; then
   252→            echo "::error::No Desktop E2E run found (with tests actually executed) for any commit in ${PREV_TAG}..HEAD. Desktop code changed but was never verified."
   253→            exit 1
   254→          fi
   255→
   256→          if [ "$LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION" != "success" ]; then
   257→            echo "::error::Most recent Desktop E2E run in range (at ${LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA}) did not succeed (conclusion: ${LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION})."
   258→            exit 1
   259→          fi
   260→
   261→          echo "Desktop E2E: passed (run ${LATEST_IN_RANGE_RUN_ID} at ${LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA} with tests executed)"
   262→
   263→      - name: Skip Desktop E2E (no desktop changes)
   264→        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--') && needs.detect-changes.outputs.desktop != 'true'
   265→        run: echo "No desktop-related changes since last release — skipping Desktop E2E gate"
   266→
   267→      - name: Summary
   268→        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--')
   269→        run: echo "All E2E gates passed — release is safe to merge."
   270→
```

> AGENT

I'll make `detect-changes` run on all PRs (removing its `if` gate), short-circuiting early for non-release PRs. Then `verify-e2e` can drop `if: "!cancelled()"` since its `needs` always succeeds.

```
     1→name: Release Gate
     2→
     3→on:
     4→  pull_request:
     5→    branches: [main]
     6→    types: [opened, synchronize, reopened]
     7→
     8→jobs:
     9→  detect-changes:
    10→    name: Detect Changes Since Last Release
    11→    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    12→    outputs:
    13→      desktop: ${{ steps.check.outputs.desktop }}
    14→      prev_tag: ${{ steps.check.outputs.prev_tag }}
    15→      is_release: ${{ steps.release-check.outputs.is_release }}
    16→    steps:
    17→      - name: Check if release PR
    18→        id: release-check
    19→        run: |
    20→          if [[ "${{ github.head_ref }}" == release-please--* ]]; then
    21→            echo "is_release=true" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    22→          else
    23→            echo "is_release=false" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    24→            echo "Not a release PR — skipping change detection"
    25→          fi
    26→
    27→      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
    28→        if: steps.release-check.outputs.is_release == 'true'
    29→        with:
    30→          fetch-depth: 0
    31→
    32→      - name: Check for desktop-related changes since last release
    33→        if: steps.release-check.outputs.is_release == 'true'
    34→        id: check
    35→        run: |
    36→          # Find the latest non-staging release tag
    37→          PREV_TAG=$(git tag --list 'v*' --sort=-v:refname \
    38→            | grep -v 'staging' \
    39→            | head -1)
    40→
    41→          if [ -z "$PREV_TAG" ]; then
    42→            echo "No previous release tag found — assuming desktop changed"
    43→            echo "desktop=true" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    44→            echo "prev_tag=" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    45→            exit 0
    46→          fi
    47→
    48→          echo "Previous release tag: ${PREV_TAG}"
    49→          echo "prev_tag=${PREV_TAG}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    50→          echo "Diffing ${PREV_TAG}..HEAD"
    51→
    52→          # Desktop-related path patterns (mirrored from e2e-desktop.yml)
    53→          DESKTOP_PATTERNS=(
    54→            'apps/desktop/src/'
    55→            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/'
    56→            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/vendor/'
    57→            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/capabilities/'
    58→            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/resources/'
    59→            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/Cargo.toml'
    60→            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/Cargo.lock'
    61→            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/build.rs'
    62→            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/rust-toolchain.toml'
    63→            'apps/desktop/index.html'
    64→            'apps/desktop/vite.config.'
    65→            'apps/desktop/tsconfig'
    66→            'packages/crypto/src/'
    67→            'packages/crypto/tsconfig'
    68→            '.github/workflows/e2e-desktop.yml'
    69→          )
    70→
    71→          CHANGED_FILES=$(git diff --name-only "${PREV_TAG}..HEAD")
    72→          DESKTOP_CHANGED=false
    73→
    74→          for pattern in "${DESKTOP_PATTERNS[@]}"; do
    75→            if echo "$CHANGED_FILES" | grep -Fq "$pattern"; then
    76→              echo "Desktop change detected matching pattern: ${pattern}"
    77→              DESKTOP_CHANGED=true
    78→              break
    79→            fi
    80→          done
    81→
    82→          echo "desktop=${DESKTOP_CHANGED}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    83→          echo "Desktop changes detected: ${DESKTOP_CHANGED}"
    84→
    85→  verify-e2e:
    86→    name: Verify E2E Passed
    87→    needs: detect-changes
    88→    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    89→    timeout-minutes: 40
    90→    steps:
    91→      - name: Skip (not a release PR)
    92→        if: needs.detect-changes.outputs.is_release != 'true'
    93→        run: echo "Not a release PR — E2E gate not applicable"
    94→
    95→      - name: Wait for Web E2E and verify it passed

Wait — those outputs need to go through the `check` step ID to be picked up by the job outputs. Let me check how the job outputs are wired.

The job outputs reference `steps.check.outputs.desktop` and `steps.check.outputs.prev_tag`, but `steps.release-check.outputs.*` for `is_release`. For defaults on non-release PRs, I need to either add them to the `release-check` step ID outputs or update the job output references. The simplest: add `desktop` and `prev_tag` as outputs from the `release-check` step too, and update the job outputs to fall back.

  "new_string": "  verify-e2e:\n    name: Verify E2E Passed\n    needs: detect-changes\n    if: \"!cancelled()\"\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    timeout-minutes: 40\n    steps:\n      - name: Fail if detect-changes did not complete (release PR)\n        if: needs.detect-changes.outputs.is_release == 'true' && needs.detect-changes.result != 'success'\n        run: |\n          echo \"::error::detect-changes must succeed before E2E verification.\"\n          exit 1\n\n      - name: Skip (not a release PR)\n        if: needs.detect-changes.outputs.is_release != 'true'\n        run: echo \"Not a release PR — E2E gate not applicable\""

     1→name: Release Gate
     2→
     3→on:
     4→  pull_request:
     5→    branches: [main]
     6→    types: [opened, synchronize, reopened]
     7→
     8→jobs:
     9→  detect-changes:
    10→    name: Detect Changes Since Last Release
    11→    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    12→    outputs:
    13→      desktop: ${{ steps.check.outputs.desktop || steps.release-check.outputs.desktop }}
    14→      prev_tag: ${{ steps.check.outputs.prev_tag || steps.release-check.outputs.prev_tag }}
    15→      is_release: ${{ steps.release-check.outputs.is_release }}
    16→    steps:
    17→      - name: Check if release PR
    18→        id: release-check
    19→        run: |
    20→          if [[ "${{ github.head_ref }}" == release-please--* ]]; then
    21→            echo "is_release=true" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    22→          else
    23→            echo "is_release=false" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    24→            echo "desktop=false" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    25→            echo "prev_tag=" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    26→            echo "Not a release PR — skipping change detection"
    27→          fi
    28→
    29→      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
    30→        if: steps.release-check.outputs.is_release == 'true'
    31→        with:
    32→          fetch-depth: 0
    33→
    34→      - name: Check for desktop-related changes since last release
    35→        if: steps.release-check.outputs.is_release == 'true'
    36→        id: check
    37→        run: |
    38→          # Find the latest non-staging release tag
    39→          PREV_TAG=$(git tag --list 'v*' --sort=-v:refname \
    40→            | grep -v 'staging' \
    41→            | head -1)
    42→
    43→          if [ -z "$PREV_TAG" ]; then
    44→            echo "No previous release tag found — assuming desktop changed"
    45→            echo "desktop=true" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    46→            echo "prev_tag=" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    47→            exit 0
    48→          fi
    49→
    50→          echo "Previous release tag: ${PREV_TAG}"
    51→          echo "prev_tag=${PREV_TAG}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    52→          echo "Diffing ${PREV_TAG}..HEAD"
    53→
    54→          # Desktop-related path patterns (mirrored from e2e-desktop.yml)
    55→          DESKTOP_PATTERNS=(
    56→            'apps/desktop/src/'
    57→            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/'
    58→            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/vendor/'
    59→            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/capabilities/'
    60→            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/resources/'
    61→            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/Cargo.toml'
    62→            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/Cargo.lock'
    63→            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/build.rs'
    64→            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/rust-toolchain.toml'
    65→            'apps/desktop/index.html'
    66→            'apps/desktop/vite.config.'
    67→            'apps/desktop/tsconfig'
    68→            'packages/crypto/src/'
    69→            'packages/crypto/tsconfig'
    70→            '.github/workflows/e2e-desktop.yml'
    71→          )
    72→
    73→          CHANGED_FILES=$(git diff --name-only "${PREV_TAG}..HEAD")
    74→          DESKTOP_CHANGED=false
    75→
    76→          for pattern in "${DESKTOP_PATTERNS[@]}"; do
    77→            if echo "$CHANGED_FILES" | grep -Fq "$pattern"; then
    78→              echo "Desktop change detected matching pattern: ${pattern}"
    79→              DESKTOP_CHANGED=true
    80→              break
    81→            fi
    82→          done
    83→
    84→          echo "desktop=${DESKTOP_CHANGED}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    85→          echo "Desktop changes detected: ${DESKTOP_CHANGED}"
    86→
    87→  verify-e2e:
    88→    name: Verify E2E Passed
    89→    needs: detect-changes
    90→    if: "!cancelled()"
    91→    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    92→    timeout-minutes: 40
    93→    steps:
    94→      - name: Fail if detect-changes did not complete (release PR)
    95→        if: needs.detect-changes.outputs.is_release == 'true' && needs.detect-changes.result != 'success'
    96→        run: |
    97→          echo "::error::detect-changes must succeed before E2E verification."
    98→          exit 1
    99→
   100→      - name: Skip (not a release PR)

> AGENT

The reviewer wants `always()` explicitly. While `!cancelled()` should work per GitHub docs (it's a status check function that overrides implicit `success()`), using `always() && !cancelled()` removes all ambiguity.

  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/.github/workflows/release-gate.yml",
  "old_string": "    if: \"!cancelled()\"",
  "new_string": "    if: always() && !cancelled()"
}

> AGENT

Valid point — if `detect-changes` fails before producing outputs, `is_release` is empty and the skip path runs. Fix: use `github.head_ref` directly in step conditions.
